Plan: both move into a foreground service (types microphone, camera) started when Record is pressed (Android allows camera / microphone only when started from the screen); the recording goes on when the app is minimised or the phone is locked, until Stop (the note, the notification, the floating button) or the time limit (D251) ends it; the floating Stop (D249 switch) for audio and camera too; the screen recording keeps stopping when the screen is locked (Android ends a capture then anyway). Also answered: an audio note records the microphone only; the choice microphone / system / both exists for camera video and screen recording. | DONE 0.9.66 (`RecordingKeepAlive` / `AndroidRecordingKeepAlive` / `RecordingService` (types microphone and camera from Android 11, a channel "Recordings", Stop in the notification, the floating Stop of D249 when its switch is on and the overlay is allowed, `stopRequests` to the recorder; a Stop with nobody listening ends the service); `AudioViewModel`: starts it with a recording, `onScreenStopped` no longer ends a recording that is kept alive (still does when Android refused the service), stops it on every end; `CameraCapture`: CameraX bound to its own `CameraLifecycle`, which follows the screen but stays resumed while a video is recorded (so the camera closes in the background when nothing records: battery, privacy), the service started with the video and stopped at its Finalize; `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CAMERA`; the floating Stop switch also in camera video notes; test `AudioViewModelTest` +1. Whether CameraX keeps recording with the preview surface gone on a locked phone must be checked on the phone.) |
| D254 | **Managing the app log in the settings (user, 2026-09-27: "add to the settings a point to manage the logger: on / off, the logging levels, and an automatic clearing, e.g. once a day, a week, a month, so the log does not overflow").** Plan: Settings -> About -> App log gets: a switch "Keep an app log" (on by default; off: nothing is written, FATAL and the error window of D224 still work, as they are about the crash itself), the lowest level kept (INFO / WARN / ERROR / CRITICAL; INFO by default), and "Clear by itself": never / daily / weekly / monthly (weekly suggested), done by the once-a-day housekeeping of D221 (no job of its own, rule 18). Already true today: the log never overflows, it is capped at 256 KB with one previous file kept. Open question (asked when the item starts): whether the error window / FATAL lines also follow the switch. **Answered 2026-09-27:** crashes are always written (user: "crashes always"); clearing by itself **once a day** by default; the levels are **ticks, each on its own, all ticked by default** (user: "the level filters are chosen with ticks, all of them: critical, warning, info, error, debug — what else do we have? write everything for now"). Built in 0.9.59: `AppLog.Options` (enabled, the ticked levels, the clearing period), kept in the log's own small preferences file and read once at the start; a new level **DEBUG** (none written yet); `accepts` (FATAL always); the clearing by itself checked when a line is written (one comparison, no job); Settings → Help and about → App log: "Keep an app log", ticks DEBUG / INFO / WARN / ERROR / CRITICAL with FATAL shown ticked and fixed, "Clear by itself" never / daily / weekly / monthly, a note that the log never takes more than about half a megabyte. | DONE 0.9.59 |
| D253 | **Search in the trash (user, 2026-09-27: "add search to the trash, there are often many items too").** Plan: a magnifier in the top bar of the trash; the same search as everywhere (D238 / D246: the words marked, "3 / 7", previous / next, the ticks "any case" and "Regexp", wildcards) over the deleted folders and notes (names, and the text of notes) and the deleted media items (names, comments). | DONE 0.9.62 (the shared `NoteSearch` / `NoteSearchBar` with the hint "Find in the trash"; `TextSearch.snippet` shows the piece of a note's text around the current match; `TrashRow.FolderRow.noteTitles`, `TrashRow.MediaRow.comment`; all rows stay listed, the arrows scroll to the match; tests `TextSearchTest` +1, `AppFlowTest.theSearchOfTheTrashFindsTheTextOfADeletedNote`) |
| D252 | **Search in the calendar (user, 2026-09-27: "the calendar needs a search too: find in it every note, reminder, in short the linked items, the same way of searching").** Plan: a magnifier in the calendar's top bar; the same search (D238 / D246) over what the calendar shows — the note names of reminders (later also budget merges D237 and tasks D239) — across months, not only the one shown: a list of the found items with their dates, a tap goes to that day (the month changes), the arrows step from one to the next. | DONE 0.9.62 (`CalendarPlan.all`: each reminder once at its (next) time, done ones too, and each budget merge; `CalendarViewModel.everything`, `showDayOf`; `NoteSearch.jumpTo`; the found list under the month, a tap or the arrows change the month and the day; tests `CalendarPlanTest` +1, `AppFlowTest.theSearchOfTheCalendarGoesToTheMonthOfWhatItFinds`) |
| D251 | **A time limit for every recording (user, 2026-09-27: "if Android cannot show the Stop button, the minimised app records until we open it and press Stop; I suggest a limit, 4 minutes by default, any value or 0 = until stopped; for every recording; set in the settings (our gear), a list of recording settings, all 10 languages; a line per kind — audio recording length 4 minutes (0 = until stopped, 1 - 65535), and a choice seconds / minutes / hours, minutes by default; also for video and screen recording, 4 minutes everywhere by default; in the notes, when Record is pressed, a warning that there is a limit of N minutes / seconds / hours; Stop still works earlier; the limit is safety / reliability").** Built in 0.9.56: `domain/model/RecordingLimit` (amount 0 - 65535, unit, 4 min by default, stored as `4:MINUTES`); a new settings page **Recordings** (between Notes and Security) with three lines — audio, camera video, screen recording — each a number field and seconds / minutes / hours chips, searchable; the audio note, the camera video and the screen recording note show "Stops by itself after 4 min (Settings → Recordings)" (or "No time limit") under the Record button and say "The recording stops by itself after …" when it starts; at the limit the recording is stopped and **saved** the ordinary way, with "The time limit was reached: the recording is stopped and saved." — audio by a timer of its view model (the voice recording lives only while its screen is open), camera video by CameraX's own duration limit (the file is finished cleanly; ERROR_DURATION_LIMIT_REACHED is not a failure), screen recording by a delayed stop in the service (removed by any other stop). Rule 18: no polling, one timer per running recording, cancelled with it. | DONE 0.9.56 |
| D250 | **Quick scroll to the beginning and the end (user, 2026-09-27: "in folders make buttons for a quick scroll to the end and to the beginning of the notes: with a long feed one needs to go up or down fast; also in the notes: lists, photo, audio, video recording, screen recording, budget").** Plan: two small round buttons (to the top ⤒, to the bottom ⤓) at the side of the notes of an open folder on the main screen and in list, photo, audio, camera video, screen recording and budget notes; shown only when the list is longer than the screen and while it scrolls (they fade after a moment, so they never cover the content for long); one tap jumps at once (an animated scroll for a short list). Answered 2026-09-27: both buttons, each while there is somewhere to go; and "in all Views of all notes and in all list notes, and in the Edit of the text and Markdown notes too when the text is long". Built in 0.9.54: `core/ui/QuickScroll` (for a lazy list and for a scrolling column: shown while it moves and 2.5 s after, each button only when it has somewhere to go, a far jump instant and a near one animated; `QuickScrollAlways` for the Edit field of a long text — over 3 000 characters or 40 lines — which moves the cursor to the start / the end so the field follows); used on the main screen (above the New note button), in list (View, Edit), budget, schedule, audio, photo, camera video and screen recording notes, the View of Markdown and "Note" notes and the Edit of both. | DONE 0.9.54 |
| D249 | **Screen recording stops after a few seconds on the phone, serious (user, 2026-09-27: "screen recording crashes, maybe the floating Stop button is the reason: on the emulator there is no such button and it does not crash; it crashes after a couple of seconds, the Stop button manages to appear on the phone, then the button disappears and the recording stops").** Same report as D230 (Samsung A52s, 3 - 4 s). What is known: the emulator has no overlay permission, so no floating button, and works; on the phone the button appears and then the service ends. The log of D230 names who stopped the recording ("the floating Stop button", "the Stop button (notification or note)", "Android stopped the screen capture (N s after the start)", "the time limit of Android", "the recording service was ended while recording") and why the previous run ended (native crash, ANR, killed for memory). Plan: (1) the user sends Settings -> About -> App log right after one failed attempt; (2) a switch "Floating Stop button" in the settings of the screen recording, to try without it at once (useful anyway); (3) fix by the log: e.g. a false tap (ACTION_UP without a move right after the button appears), the overlay window of a service without the flags a capture needs, Android 14 stopping the projection when an overlay covers the screen, or a crash in the overlay; (4) a test of the button's touch handling. **The log (user, 2026-09-27, Samsung SM-A528B, Android 14, 0.9.54):** "recording" 51.702 (the floating button was added right before it), "Android stopped the screen capture (0.8 s after the start)" 51.913 — 0.2 s after the button appeared; the user pressed nothing ("the Stop button was there and just disappeared"). So Android itself ends the capture when the app's overlay window appears over the captured screen. Built in 0.9.55: (1) the floating button is shown **before** the capture starts; (2) a switch "Floating Stop button" next to the sound choice of a screen recording note (on by default; off: a hint that Stop is in the notification); (3) self-healing: if Android stops the capture within 5 s of the button appearing, the log says so (CRITICAL), the button is turned off for the next recordings and a message tells the person; (4) the button is removed whatever ends the service. Whether (1) alone fixes the Samsung is **not known yet**: the next attempt on the phone decides; if it still stops, (3) turns the button off and the recording after works without it. **Second log (0.9.55, 2026-09-27 13:08):** the button shown before the capture did **not** help: Android stopped the capture 0.5 - 0.6 s after the start, twice; the self-healing logged "the button is turned off", yet the next attempt 15 s later started with the button again ("wanted true") — only the screen's copy of the switch reached the service. Fixed in 0.9.56: the service reads the saved switch itself at the start. **Still open:** whether the button is the cause at all; the next attempt runs without it (turned off by the app) and decides; if it still stops, the next suspects are the capture of the phone's sound (try "no sound") and the step aside of the app right after the start (now in the log). **The likely cause, found in the code (0.9.57):** while the app lock is on, the window of Notio has `FLAG_SECURE` (no screenshots, no picture in the recent apps). The capture starts while that window is still on the screen (the app steps aside at the same moment), and a Samsung with Android 14 ends a capture that has a secure window in it — which also explains why the floating button did not matter (without it the recording stopped too, D230) and why the emulator (no lock) works. Fix: `ScreenRecordService.capturing` is set from the moment a capture is started (screen, and the phone's sound for a camera video) until it ends by any path; while it is set the window is not secure (`windowSecure(lockOn, capturing)`); after the capture the protection comes back. The log now says when the window becomes secure / not secure. The floating button stays switchable; the self-healing turned it off on the user's phone, and it can be turned on again. **To be confirmed on the phone.** **Third log (0.9.57) and the answer (user, 2026-09-27: "my mistake, I had not switched off the restriction on the Samsung"; "I had set hard background restrictions by accident, that is why it could not record"):** the capture stopped without the button, without sound and with the window not secure — none of the three guesses was the cause; the phone's battery settings had Notio "Restricted" in the background (the log had said "background restricted" at an earlier exit). **0.9.58:** the guesses of 0.9.55 - 0.9.57 that changed behaviour are taken back (the window is secure again whenever the lock is on; the self-healing that turned the button off is removed); the button before the capture and its switch stay. New: `core/common/BackgroundRestriction` — before a screen recording a restricted Notio shows a dialog (why it would stop; "Open the settings" → the app's page, Battery → Unrestricted; "Record anyway"); a capture that Android ends within 5 s while restricted is explained by a message and a CRITICAL log line; Settings → Recordings shows "Work in the background: allowed / restricted"; the start of a recording logs the restriction. | DONE 0.9.58 |
| D248 | **Search inside audio, photo, camera video and screen recording notes (user, 2026-09-27: "add search by names and comments for audio notes, photo notes, screen recordings and video"; "records, audio, video and photo can be searched by the name of the item — is the comment useful?").** Answer given: every item of these kinds already has a name and a comment, and the search of notes on the main screen already reads both; the search inside the note will read both too, as in lists and budgets. Plan: the magnifier also on these four kinds; matches marked in the names and comments of the items, the list of items scrolls to the current one; the ticks of D246 apply. Built in 0.9.53: `core/ui/NoteSearch` (`ItemHitKey`, `SearchableItem`, `itemHits`, `ItemSearchEffects`); `AudioScreen` and `MediaNoteScreen` (photo, camera video, screen recording) report the matches in the names as shown (a default name like "Recording 3" when there is none) and the comments, mark them and scroll to the current item; the magnifier is shown for these four kinds. | DONE 0.9.53 |
| D247 | **Mistakes of a regular expression told clearly, in every language, with their place (user, 2026-09-27: "it is not quite clear when it gives a syntax error with Regexp; we have 10 languages and must tell the person properly that they made a mistake in the expression, and if possible which and where").** The engines word their errors only in English and differently (ICU on the phone, the JVM in tests), so `domain/model/PatternCheck` reads the expression itself and names the usual mistakes: a quantifier with nothing to repeat (`*Tasty*`), an unclosed `(`, a `)` without `(`, an unclosed `[`, a `{` that is not a count, a `\` at the end, a backwards range (`[z-a]`); anything else the engine refuses is "cannot read this expression" with the engine's place. Under the field: "Error in the expression, character N: <what and how to write it instead>" in the language of the app, and the expression with the wrong character marked in red. The check never invents a mistake: each refused expression is refused by the engine too (tested); a leading count like `{2}a` is left to the engine. Also on 2026-09-27: the user saw "the first typed words find nothing, after deleting and typing again it finds"; not reproduced under Robolectric (typing letter by letter in both searches finds at once, test `typingTheFirstWordsLetterByLetterSearchesAtOnce`), details asked. | DONE 0.9.52 |
| D246 | **Search options: case and patterns (user, 2026-09-27: "the search needs a tick for case-insensitive search, sometimes it must be exact and sometimes not; and regexp support, in the note search too, e.g. *Tasty* finds any where it occurs; with the tick both upper and lower case; describe regexp in the help; improve the Markdown, list, budget search and the search of the notes themselves"; answered: "two ticks").** Two ticks next to the search field, both in the search inside a note (D238) and in the search of notes on the main screen: **Ignore case** (on by default, as before) and **Regexp** (off). Without Regexp the words may hold wildcards: `*` any characters (also none), `?` one character; `*Tasty*` = anything containing "Tasty". With Regexp the words are a regular expression (Java syntax); a wrong one shows a short note under the field instead of results, never a crash; an expression that matches empty text finds nothing there. The help describes both, with examples. Built in 0.9.51: `domain/model/TextMatcher.kt` (`SearchOptions`, `TextPattern`: wildcards made into a regular expression within a line, the ends trimmed; a user expression compiled with the case flag; matches found on a text that stops the engine after 0.3 s, so a runaway expression finds nothing instead of freezing the screen; empty matches skipped; at most 10 000 matches); the search inside a note (`NoteSearch` ticks, `SearchOptionsRow` under its field, the error in red) and the search of notes, folders and tags on the main screen (`SearchQuery.ignoreCase / regex`, patterns made once per search, not per note) use it; a new help page "Search: case, patterns, regexp" with examples in 10 languages. The ticks are not remembered between searches. | DONE 0.9.51 |
| D245 | **Dragging a checklist line of the Markdown View landed in the wrong place, and the first drag "went back" (user, 2026-09-27: "the order breaks: I drag it before item 255 and it becomes before 253"; "it only moves on the second try"; "the first time it goes back to its place").** Causes, each shown by a test: (1) the drag state of a group was remembered by the line numbers of its items, which stay 0, 1, 2 … after a move, so the order of the last drag was put again on the new text: a swap of two neighbours looked undone, and the next drag started from a wrong order (`aSecondDragWorksAsWellAsTheFirst` failed before the fix); (2) the scroll at the edge ran towards an edge the line was merely near, also when the finger pulled the other way, so a line taken near the top and pulled down ran upwards; (3) the few pixels before a drag is recognised (touch slop) were not counted, so the line lagged behind the finger. Fix: the state belongs to the text of the group (and the order shown goes back to the text on every drop); the edge scroll only towards where the line is pulled (`CheckDrag.edgeScroll`), 48 dp band, once per frame (`withFrameNanos`, only while a line is held); the finger followed in screen coordinates from the point where it touched the grip. Tests: `MarkdownViewChecklistTest` (precise drop on another line, a second drag, a drop near line 255 of 300 after scrolling, delete), `CheckDragTest` (edge scroll). | DONE 0.9.48 |
| D244 | **Code pass under rule 18 (user, 2026-09-27: "go through all the code after the fix: stability, reliability, battery, performance").** Swept all 211 source files for: unbounded loops (every `while (true)` has an exit: end of stream, limit, exception or `isActive`), blocking of the main thread (only the deliberate first-frame reads of D200), unchecked `!!` (all 18 guarded), coroutines without error handling around I/O, JSON of stored data (all guarded), bitmaps, cursors, media players, services, receivers, network discovery, alarms and timers (all tied to an active action: recording, playing, dragging, the lock wait; sync listens only while its page is open; exact alarms only for reminders). Fixed: (1) **a photo could crash the app or run out of memory**: `savePhoto` had no error handling (and left the camera "busy"), and `PhotoComposer` held up to three full-size copies of the picture — now decoded mutable, a picture above 24 Mpx halved, each copy freed as soon as it is not needed, a failure is "could not save" plus a log line; (2) **keeping an audio recording** could crash on a full disk — now a "recording failed" message; (3) **importing a database file** that became unreadable crashed with the database closed — now a failed import; (4) the new checklist code of D235 did a search per line (quadratic in a long list) — the items, their parents and their sibling groups are now found in one pass per text, each item's blocks split once per group. Nothing found about battery beyond what D221 / D231 fixed. | DONE 0.9.48 |
| D243 | **Rule 18: every code check covers stability / reliability, battery / economy and performance / resources (user, 2026-09-27).** Written into §1.1; the first pass under it follows D242. | ACCEPTED |
| D242 | **The app froze as soon as a checklist line of the Markdown View was dragged (user, 2026-09-27: "when dragging a list item in an md note in View the app hangs at once").** Cause: the dragged line changed places with a neighbour once it was pulled past half of the neighbour's height, and the offset left after the swap (minus height and gap) was then past half of it the other way, so the line swapped back, and so on for ever inside a `while (true)` on the main thread. Fix: the threshold is half of the whole slot (height + gap) in both directions, so a swap never makes the opposite swap due; the step is a pure function `CheckDrag.settle` with a bounded loop (at most as many moves as there are lines); tests `CheckDragTest` (the exact frozen case) and `MarkdownViewChecklistTest` (Robolectric: a real drag gesture and the delete confirmation). | DONE 0.9.47 |
| D241 | **Drawing and blueprint: zoom and fit the whole sheet, tools over the sheet (user, 2026-09-27: "add to drawings and pictures zoom and normalisation of the whole canvas, because in tablet mode the canvas can be small; the tools and buttons of course over the canvas; the canvas with the picture or vector elements on it must not cover the interface, ok?").** Today the sheet is a window of the sheet's proportions between the toolbars (above and below), clipped, zoom 1x - 8x by buttons and pinch, "100%" back to normal (D118); on a tablet the toolbars take height and the sheet stays small. Plan: the sheet takes the whole area of the note; the toolbars and buttons lie **over** the sheet (their own background, the sheet never drawn over them: the sheet is clipped to its area and sits under the tool layers); a **Fit** button shows the whole sheet as large as the area allows (on a tablet much larger than now), zoom in / out around it by buttons and two fingers, pan when zoomed. Drawing and blueprint alike, View and Edit. Open questions (asked when the item starts): may the tool panels be folded away to see the whole sheet; can zoom go below Fit (sheet smaller than the area); does "normalisation" also mean a bigger sheet size for new drawings on tablets. | DONE 0.9.63. The open questions were decided by the agent while the owner was away (2026-09-27, "do it all in a row without me"): the tool panel **can be folded** ("Hide the tools" / "Tools"); zoom does **not** go below Fit (100% = the whole sheet as large as the area allows: Fit is the percentage button, as before); the sheet size of new drawings is **unchanged**. Built: `BoxWithConstraints` in `DrawingScreen`: wide (>= 600 dp and wider than high) = sheet + a 340 dp tool column at the side; else the sheet at the top of the whole area and the tools in a `Surface` over its bottom (at most 55% of the height, scrolls), with the covered part measured; `SheetView.within` / `coveredBottom` lets the sheet move up by the covered part, also at 1x; folding moves it back. Tests `SheetViewTest` +1, `AppFlowTest.theToolPanelOfADrawingFoldsAwayAndComesBack`. |

An **Income** or **Expense** note has, besides everything an account has: the target account note (a budget note of kind Account; none chosen = nothing happens), the date and time of the merge, ticks **Sum** / **Goal** (what is carried), **+ / −** (add or subtract; + by default for Income, − for Expense), **Same items** (an item with the same name in the target gets the sum / goal added or subtracted) and **Transfer all items** (on by default: items with no match are created in the target). **Merge now** does it at once. At the date the merge runs once (exact alarm, re-armed after a reboot; if the phone was off it runs at the next start), both budget logs record it, the income / expense note goes to the trash and can be restored and scheduled again. Tests: `BudgetMergePlannerTest` (6), `BudgetMergeServiceTest` (5), `CalendarPlanTest` +1. | DONE 0.9.61 |

| D231 | **Libraries updated, a pass for speed, battery, reliability and security, and a release build (user, 2026-09-24: "update the libraries; then go through the code: performance, battery, reliability so it does not crash, and security (VirusTotal); then the docs and build a release"; signing: "for now with the debug key").** (1) AGP 8.13.0 -> **9.4.1** (built-in Kotlin: the `kotlin-android` plugin removed), Gradle 8.14.3 -> **9.8.0**, Kotlin 2.2.20 -> **2.4.20**, KSP -> **2.3.12**, Compose BOM 2025.10.00 -> **2026.09.00**, core 1.19.1, lifecycle 2.11.0, activity 1.13.0, navigation 2.10.2, Room 2.8.5, DataStore 1.2.1, coroutines 1.11.0, serialization 1.11.0, Media3 1.11.1, CameraX 1.6.2, androidx.sqlite 2.7.1, ZXing 3.5.4, Robolectric 4.17 (SQLCipher 4.19.0 was already the newest). The new Compose libraries require AGP 9.1+. (2) Reliability: a failing `appScope` job and a failing reminder broadcast are logged as CRITICAL instead of closing the app; the screen recording and camera-sound foreground starts and the reminder melody service no longer crash when Android refuses them; the microphone foreground type is only asked for on Android 11+ (on Android 10 it made screen recording with sound fail). (3) Security: the sync server accepts only local-network addresses (loopback, link-local, private IPv4, IPv6 fc00::/7, 100.64/10) — on mobile data a phone can have a public IPv6 address; the database passphrase is copied as sensitive (hidden from the clipboard preview on Android 13+); all copies go through `core/ui/copyText`. Checked with no finding: default TLS for SMTP (no trust-all), no WebView / dynamic code / exec, PendingIntents immutable, the exported receiver acts only on system actions, the lock gates everything before any note opens, FileProvider limited to four folders, no system backup of data, no wake locks, no polling loops (the loops found are stream copies and socket readers). (4) Compose 1.12 lint: the locale is read from `LocalConfiguration` (2 places); `LocalContextGetResourceValueCall` is disabled with the reason in `app/build.gradle.kts` (strings are read at a click, the activity is recreated for language / theme). Kept on purpose: Media3's deprecated `EditedMediaItemSequence.Builder(vararg)` (the new one needs the track types, to be checked on a device), the deprecated Compose test rules (v2 changes the dispatcher), `targetSdk 36` (37 = Android 17 behaviour, "at the very end"), minify off (O8). (5) Release 0.9.44 (build 61): `assembleRelease` (not debuggable), aligned and signed with the **debug key** at the user's choice, so it installs over the earlier builds; certificate SHA-256 e146abb8…b0f979, the same as before. VirusTotal: the permissions (screen capture, microphone, overlay, location, boot) are what heuristics look at; a real release key is the next step (PUBLICATION_CHECKLIST_RU). | ACCEPTED (0.9.44) |
| D230 | **The app log, and screen recording traced (user, 2026-09-24: "screen recording breaks: it starts, the Stop button appears on top and disappears, a piece of the recording is left" — Samsung A52s, no sound and system + microphone, 3 - 4 s, no error window; "I asked to cover the code with a logger, especially critical and fatal").** New `core/common/AppLog`: levels INFO / WARN / ERROR / CRITICAL / FATAL, one line each (+ the error and up to 12 frames per cause), `files/logs/app.log` (256 KB, the previous file kept as `app.1.log`), written on its own thread, FATAL at once; also to logcat as `Notio/<tag>`. Never note content, passwords, codes or keys. The Java crash handler (`CrashLog`, D203) writes FATAL first. On start, `AppLog.recordPreviousExit` reads Android's `ApplicationExitInfo` (Android 11+) and logs why each earlier run ended (native crash / ANR = FATAL, killed for memory or by a signal = CRITICAL, ordinary ends = INFO); a native crash or ANR also opens the error window (D224) at the next start, which the Java handler could not see. Traced: screen recording (start, screen / video size, foreground start, each step, MediaRecorder errors and info, Android stopping the capture with the seconds since the start, what stopped it — floating button, notification / note Stop, Android, the FGS time limit —, the sizes, the finishing, the service ended while recording, memory pressure, swiped away), camera (open failure, video finalize errors, not saved), voice recording start, overlays not applied, backups (written / restored / failed), the database file import, exact alarms not allowed, and every sync log line. Settings -> About -> **App log**: newest first, Copy (whole file), Clear, Close; searchable in the settings search. The screen recording stop itself is **not fixed yet**: the code shows no reason of its own, the log of one attempt on the A52s is needed. | ACCEPTED (0.9.43) |
| D229 | **Finding devices for sync, fixed (user, 2026-09-24: "tested the sync, everything works except the search: I waited and nothing was found, typed the address by hand"; "two tablets find each other, a phone and a tablet do not").** (1) A device waiting with a code announces itself under another DNS-SD name, "Notio <name> (pairing)", so the others see a new service; before, only the TXT record `pair` changed from 0 to 1 and the searching side kept the old record, so the Find list (which showed only waiting devices) stayed empty. The searcher keeps one entry per device id. (2) The Find dialog lists every Notio device seen, the waiting ones first and marked "waiting with a code"; the others say "not waiting: press Wait for a device there" and, if chosen, answer "not waiting" as before. (3) The service name is cut to the 63 bytes of UTF-8 a DNS label allows (a long Cyrillic device name made the announcement fail silently). (4) A failed announcement is tried again (2 times, 1.5 s apart), a busy resolve too (3 times); failures and one line per page visit ("seen A, B" / "no other Notio device was seen") go to the sync log, so a device that finds nothing can be diagnosed. The phone-to-tablet case is not confirmed fixed: it may be the network or the phone (multicast filtered); the log lines are there to tell. Bug fix of D226, no parity row. | ACCEPTED (0.9.42) |

The `Co-Authored-By` trailer was removed from every commit message of the history (rewritten with `git filter-branch --msg-filter`, commit hashes changed; the old history is kept in `../Notio-Android-backup-2026-09-24.bundle`), and git rule §1.1.1 forbids AI attribution from now on. |

Background slots ("Notes" / "Markdown notes"), the help (kinds list, Markdown notes, formatted notes, export, share) follow in all 10 languages. Only names change: `NoteType.TEXT` / `NoteType.WORD`, files and formats stay, so nothing moves in the data. Closes the "Word is a Microsoft trademark" question before publication. |
| D226 | **Sync between two Notio devices on the local network (user, 2026-09-24: "a Sync tab in the settings, a role only for setting up the connection: server and client, one waits, the other searches; one generates a key, the other types it; the connection is encrypted, kept in a list inside the Sync tab, can be seen, broken and removed, tested, diagnosed; then the sync itself: from where to where, direction, merge / union / replace"; "also choose which folders are synced — all or chosen with check boxes — in the same place; the connection is kept in the list and when the devices are in touch again they can sync: resumable after the first code exchange").** Implements §2.1 for Android. **Page** Settings -> Sync (its own section): this device (name, editable; "visible while this page is open: ip:port"); **New connection** — *Wait for a device* (server role: shows a code `XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`, 12 Crockford-base32 characters = 60 bits, and announces "waiting") / *Find a device* (client role: the devices waiting nearby, found by mDNS `_notio-sync._tcp`, or an address typed; then the code); the waiting side confirms "Connect with <name>?"; **Connections** — kept pairings with online / not seen, last sync, *Sync…*, *Test connection*, *Remove* (removed on both devices when the other is reachable); **Sync log** (last 200 lines, no secrets, copy / clear). **Sync…**: direction from here (to the other / from the other / both ways), mode as in an import (Merge — the newer change wins per record; Add as copies; Replace — the receiver becomes an exact copy, allowed only with all folders and one direction), folders (All, or check boxes over this device's folders plus the other's, fetched on demand); the choices are remembered per pairing; the other device is ASKED every time (Allow / Refuse, 90 s), with warnings for Replace and for clocks more than 2 min apart. **Nothing in the background:** the TCP port (47821, else a free one) and the announcement exist only while the page is on the screen (lifecycle START / STOP); connections are one at a time. **Protocol v1** (`data/sync/`): plain hello (version, purpose pair / sync, device id and name, fresh ECDH P-256 public key, nonce, clock, pairing id) each way; keys = HKDF-SHA256(ECDH ‖ pairing key, salt = SHA-256 of both hellos); pairing key = PBKDF2-SHA256(code, 50 000 rounds, same salt) when pairing, the stored 32-byte secret afterwards; the answering side proves first (HMAC), then the other; wrong code / unknown pairing are refused there, three wrong codes make a new code; after that every frame is AES-256-GCM with a direction byte and a counter nonce: JSON messages (pair-ok with the new pairing id and secret, request, decision, result, list-folders / folders, ping / pong, unpair, bye, error) and data chunks of 64 KB + end mark. **Data** is the ordinary backup stream without password (`BackupService.export(folderIds = …)` — new filter: chosen folders, their notes, budget nodes, reminders, trash rows and budget log), received into a temp file and imported with `importFrom` in the chosen mode; results (folders / notes added, updated, unchanged, files) go back and are shown on both sides. **Storage** (`SyncStore`, private preferences, not in backups): device id (random), name, pairings (secret sealed with the Keystore), log. **Test connection**: Wi-Fi, listening, found (mDNS / last address), TCP connect (ms), handshake, clock difference, answer (ms), with Copy. Tests: `SyncEngineTest` (two engines over a real local socket: pairing, wrong code, not waiting, both ways with folders, receive only, refusal, replace rules, test, folders, unpair, unknown pairing) and a filtered-export test. Limits: one local network only (another network would need a relay = cloud, rule 14); Windows / Linux will speak the same protocol. |
| D225 | **SQLCipher's licence in the app (user, 2026-09-24: "mention SQLCipher if its licence requires it, and tick it as done").** The Community Edition licence (BSD-style, © 2008-2026 ZETETIC LLC) asks for its notice "in the application interface and/or materials", in a user-accessible place. Settings -> About now has a credit line (SQLCipher, Roboto) and a **Licences** button that shows the full texts from `assets/licenses/` (`SQLCipher-LICENSE.txt` added next to `Roboto-OFL.txt`), read only when opened. NOTICE.md, PUBLICATION_CHECKLIST_RU.md and TODO updated. Also O2 of the security review: saving the recovery mail with "no encryption" (usually port 25) now asks "Save mail without encryption?" with the warning (user, 2026-09-24: "yes, show a warning"). |
| D224 | **The error window at the moment of the error (user, 2026-09-24: "if we have an error, a window with the error over the app, with a Close button of course, but mainly a Copy error text button, so it can be passed to you").** Before, D203 wrote the error to `last_error.txt` and showed it only at the NEXT start. Now the uncaught-exception handler writes the file and at once starts `core/common/ErrorActivity` — in its own process `:error` (the app's process is ending), translucent theme, so only the dialog is seen over the screen: title "Notio closed because of an error", the text (selectable, scrollable, monospace), **Copy error text**, **Open Notio again**, **Close**; tapping outside does not close it; closing forgets the text. Then the failing process ends quietly (no system "app has stopped" dialog on top). If the window cannot be opened (the app was in the background: Android blocks starting a screen) the old path stays: the system closes the app, the next start shows `LastErrorDialog` (same dialog, `ErrorReportDialog`). The `:error` process skips `AppContainer` (no database, reminders, housekeeping); an error inside it does not reopen the window (no loop). Costs nothing until an error happens: no service, no polling. |
| D223 | **Cloud backups removed (user, 2026-09-24: "Google: could not sign in, 8, UNREGISTERED; Yandex says the app must be registered … commit, write the results into a separate file about the Yandex and Google integration, and then, alas, we remove this functionality completely: neither Yandex nor Google, we only spent time").** Removed: the Cloud backups block of Settings -> Backup and restore, `data/cloud/*`, `CloudBackups` + `CloudBackupWorker`, `CloudBackup` model, the libraries play-services-auth, Yandex authsdk and WorkManager, the Yandex client id in the build (`buildConfig` off again), 52 strings per language, the cloud sentence of the help, the setup guide; tests of the feature. The places / accounts kept on a phone are deleted once at start (`SettingsDataStore.forgetCloudBackups`, housekeeping flag). Kept: export / import of all data to a file (with a password) — the file can be put into any cloud by hand (Share, the file picker); the D221 battery work. Results and lessons: `docs/CLOUD_INTEGRATION_RU.md`; code: commit 4fbd00f. D217 - D220 are superseded. APK 64.5 -> 61.0 MB. |
| D222 | **Photos, video and audio stay files next to the database, not BLOBs in it (recommendation, 2026-09-24; user: "where do photos, video, audio go? into the database? … better to keep everything in the database, we will make Windows and Linux clones"). PROPOSED — waiting for the user.** Today: the database holds the notes and a key per media item; the bytes are files in the app's private storage (`files/media`, `files/audio`, `files/drawings`, `files/drawings-vector`, `files/backgrounds`), invisible to other apps, removed with the app, encrypted by the phone's file-based encryption. Why not BLOBs: Android's SQLite cursor window is 2 MB per row — a photo or a clip in a row cannot even be read back through Room without splitting it into chunks; the player and the camera need a file (seeking in a video); every change rewrites pages of one growing database (WAL, VACUUM, slower backup and slower queries of all notes); a damaged database would take every picture with it. Portability to Windows / Linux does not need BLOBs: the exchange format is the backup file (`.json.zip`, optionally `.enc`) that already carries the database content and every media file together; the desktop apps use the same layout (SQLite + a media folder) and import / export the same file (their D07). |
| D221 | **Battery, speed and robustness pass (user, 2026-09-24: "we must not eat the battery … goals: 1) performance 2) reliability 3) security 4) moderate in the foreground, very economical in the background").** Audit of everything that runs without the person: no repeating alarms, no wake locks, no services except while a reminder rings / the screen is recorded, WorkManager only for cloud backups (network, battery not low, storage not low; no retries), reminders are exact alarms only at their time. Fixed: (1) the cloud settings asked the Keystore for the backup password every second while the page was open — now a preferences listener, nothing decrypted (`BackupPasswordStore.activeChanges`); (2) every process start (also one made only to ring a reminder or after a reboot) purged both trashes and scanned all notes for the D214 move — now the purge runs at most once a day and the move once ever (`housekeeping` preferences); (3) the background picture was decoded from disk on every screen — now once, kept in `ThumbnailCache`; (4) a cloud backup exported all data before checking access — now access first (no export for nothing when signed out / offline); left-over temp files older than 6 h are removed; (5) Restore downloads into its own temp folder (it deleted a running backup's temp file). Also: Google "status 8, UNREGISTERED_ON_API_CONSOLE" is read as "not registered" like status 10, Test connection checks the Google registration in its "setup" step, the "not switched on yet" texts say it is a one-time step of the developer, not of the person. |
| D220 | **Cloud backups through the account: sign in to Google / Yandex, rotation, diagnostics (user, 2026-09-23: "where is the sign-in to the Google and Yandex account? sync to Yandex Disk and Google Drive means integrating them, so signing in"; "also make a diagnostics of Yandex Disk and Google Drive, a test, so that it is clear what is wrong"; "rotation, 5 copies by default, a choice from 1 to 9 for each, separately for Yandex and Google"; "once a day, once a week, once a month, a choice").** D218 (a folder picked through the cloud's own app) is kept as "Other way"; the main way is now signing in. **Google Drive:** Play services `AuthorizationClient` (play-services-auth 22.0.0): the phone's own account chooser and consent screen, scope `drive.file` only (Notio sees only files it made), Play services keep and renew the token, a fresh one is asked before every backup (also in the background; if a screen is needed the backup fails with "sign in again"); REST v3: folder "Notio backups" found or made, resumable upload streamed from a temp file, list / download / delete; Sign out revokes the access. **Yandex Disk:** the official LoginSDK 3.2.1 (the Yandex app's account when installed, else its web page), scope app folder (`app:/` = "Applications/Notio"); the token sealed with the Keystore (`KeystoreSecretBox`) in the target; REST v1 (upload href, list, download href, delete permanently). **Per cloud:** Automatically — Off / Once a day / Once a week / Once a month (drop-down, WorkManager with network required); **Keep copies 1..9, 5 by default** (`CloudTarget.keep`, the older of Notio's own `Notio-backup-*.json.zip.enc` deleted after a new one; nothing else is touched); Back up now; Restore — the list of backups in the cloud, pick one, it is downloaded and handed to the ordinary import (asks how to import and the password); **Test connection** — steps with ✓ / ✗ and the error text: internet (DNS of the API host), app set up (Yandex client id in the build), way, account, access permission (fresh token), cloud answers (account name), backup folder (count), write / read / compare / delete `Notio-connection-test.txt`, chosen place (other way), backup password; "the first ✗ shows what is wrong", Copy report. **Setup (owner, once):** Google Cloud project with Drive API, consent screen (test users while Testing), an Android OAuth client for `com.notebudget.app` + SHA-1 of the signing key; oauth.yandex.ru app, Android platform with the package + SHA-256, permission `cloud_api:disk.app_folder`, its ClientID in `local.properties` as `yandex.clientId` (not committed; manifest placeholder `YANDEX_CLIENT_ID`, `BuildConfig.YANDEX_CLIENT_ID`). Without it: Google answers DEVELOPER_ERROR 10 shown as "not set up", the Yandex button is off with a hint. All in `docs/CLOUD_SETUP_RU.md`. Backups stay password-protected always (D218). APK +2.7 MB (Play services auth, Yandex SDK). |
| D219 | **More colour themes and any colour by RGB (user, 2026-09-23: "extend the colour schemes, add our RGB tool to pick any colour, and three bright saturated ones: yellow, lilac, magenta").** Settings -> Appearance -> Colour theme now has fourteen: the ten of D105 / D115 plus **Yellow**, **Lilac**, **Magenta** and **Your colour** with an **RGB…** button that opens the same RGB picker as notes and folders (`ColorPickerDialog`); picking a colour switches to it. Yellow: a bright yellow accent cannot be read on a white page, so — like the white theme — on light the containers (chips, cards, selected items) are bright yellow and the accent text / lines a deep gold, on dark the accent itself is bright yellow with dark text on it. **Your colour** is built by `customThemeColors`: hue and saturation of the picked colour are kept, and each shade is moved in lightness until it passes the same contrast rules as the ready themes (text on the accent 4.5 : 1, text on the light container 7 : 1, dark container 4.5 : 1, the accent on its page 4.5 : 1), so no colour — pale, dark or grey — gives an unreadable app. The picked colour is kept in the settings and in backups (`SettingsRecord.customThemeColor`). `ColorThemeTest` checks the new palettes and 304 picked colours (300 random, plus white, black, grey and pure yellow). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.37 |
| D218 | **Cloud backups, stage 1 of D217, built (user, 2026-09-23: "automatic copies chosen from a drop-down: once a day, week, month; a Back up now button and a Restore button; the same options for Google and for Yandex; a convenient place in Settings").** Settings -> Backup and restore -> **Cloud backups**: two cards, **Google Drive** and **Yandex Disk**, each with: the place (**Choose a folder** — a new file per backup, `Notio-backup-yyyy-MM-dd-HHmm.json.zip.enc`, the newest 5 of Notio's own kept, nothing else in the folder touched; or **Choose a file** — overwritten each time), chosen in the phone's picker where the Drive / Yandex Disk apps offer their storage, the permission kept across restarts; **Automatically: Off / Once a day / Once a week / Once a month** (a drop-down; WorkManager periodic work, battery and storage not low); **Back up now** (runs in the app scope, survives leaving the page); **Restore** (the newest backup of the folder, or the file, handed to the ordinary import with its mode and password); a status line (last backup time and size, or the error in red). No account, no Google / Yandex library, no network code: the cloud apps upload. **A cloud backup always uses the backup password** (the recommendation of D217; without it the buttons are off and a red line says where to set it). Places are per phone and not part of backups. New dependency `androidx.work:work-runtime-ktx` 2.10.5 (Apache 2.0, AndroidX). Help (Backup) extended in ten languages. Tests: `CloudBackupTest` (3), `AppFlowTest.aCloudBackupWithoutTheBackupPasswordIsRefusedAndSaysWhy`. **Not tested on a device** (whether Drive / Yandex Disk offer folders or only files in the picker decides which of the two ways works there). Stage 2 (direct APIs) stays in D217, waiting. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.36 |
| D217 | **Backups to Google Drive and Yandex Disk — a PLAN, not built (user, 2026-09-23: "think through backup integration with Google Drive and Yandex Disk: upload the database to the chosen storage and load it back; for now make a plan").** Stage 0 (works today, to be checked on the phone): Settings -> Data -> Export all data opens the system file picker; with the Google Drive or Yandex Disk app installed, "Drive" / "Yandex Disk" are places in it, so a backup can be saved there and imported back from there — no account in Notio, no network code. Stage 1 (recommended next, still no accounts and no SDK): a "Cloud backup" block — choose the place once (the picker; the permission is kept across restarts), then "Back up now" in one tap and an optional automatic backup (WorkManager: daily / weekly, on Wi-Fi, while charging) into that place, **always protected with the backup password** (D134) because the file sits on a third party's server; keep the last N copies where the provider lets the app list a folder; restore = the existing import from that place; a status line (last backup, size, error text). Whether Drive / Yandex allow a kept folder permission (tree access) or only a kept file must be tested on the phone first; with a kept file only, stage 1 overwrites one file. Stage 2 (optional, only if stage 1 is not enough): direct APIs — Google Drive REST v3 with the `drive.file` scope (only files the app made) through Google's authorization library, and Yandex Disk REST with OAuth (an app folder only, `cloud_api:disk.app_folder`); tokens sealed with the Keystore; resumable / chunked upload for multi-GB backups. Costs: Google Play services and Yandex ID in the app (F-Droid would need a flavour without them), app registrations and consent screens, and it relaxes D16 ("no cloud") — so opt-in only. What is uploaded: the full backup (ZIP with media, streamed, D174), not the raw database file (that one is for moving an encrypted database, D184). **Open questions for the user:** stage 1 only, or stage 2 too; how often automatically; may a cloud backup ever be without a password. | PENDING — plan written, waiting for the user's choice |
| D216 | **Share a note (user, 2026-09-23: "share a note and choose the app — Telegram, WhatsApp, MAX, imo, anything — in the note menu").** The ⋮ menu of a note card has **Share…**: Android's own share sheet, which lists every installed app that takes the content, so no messenger is built in (`feature/notes/NoteShare.kt`). Text, Word, list, budget and schedule notes go as text (title + the TXT / snapshot export); drawings and blueprints as a PNG; photo, video, screen and audio notes as their files, all items at once (`ACTION_SEND_MULTIPLE`), straight from the app's storage through the FileProvider (new paths `audio/`, `media/`, cache `shared/`) with a one-share read permission, nothing copied; a media note without items says there is nothing to share. Help section "Sharing a note" in ten languages. Test: `AppFlowTest.sharingANoteHandsItsTextOrFilesToTheShareSheet`. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.35 |
| D215 | **The budget's Change dialog: symbols only, and the sum, the goal or both (user, 2026-09-23).** The mode buttons show only **+ − =** (the words stay for TalkBack), also in the list's Change dialogs. For a budget item two switches, **Sum** (on at first) and **Goal**: the sum only, the goal only, or both — never neither (the last one on cannot be switched off); with both on the same action is applied to both (e.g. −1000 lowers both). The dialog shows both numbers now and both results ("Will be: Sum …, Goal …"). `BudgetEditor.adjustLeaf` does it in one write and logs a SUM and / or a GOAL line (ADD / SUBTRACT / SET), one undo step. Help of the budget updated in ten languages. Tests: `DialogInputTest.change_canActOnTheSumTheGoalOrBoth_neverOnNeither`, `BudgetUndoTest` (sum / goal / both / neither). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.35 |
| D214 | **Formatted text becomes its own kind of note, "Word" (user, 2026-09-23: "separate the Markdown text notes and the formatted ones, call them Word (in English); better to develop two separate kinds"; replaces the per-note switch of D211 / D213).** New kind `WORD` (label "Word" in all ten languages, its own background slot "Word notes", in the new-note dialog, in the footer-stamp choices, undo / redo and zoom like text): the formatted editor of D211 with its word-processor toolbar under the text, no switch. `TEXT` is Markdown only again, no switch either. Text notes made formatted in 0.9.32 - 0.9.33 are moved to `WORD` once when the app starts (same text and styles; `AppContainer.moveFormattedTextNotesToWord`); a formatted TEXT note that comes back in an old backup still opens with the formatted editor. Export of a Word note: Markdown / TXT / PDF through its Markdown form (`WordTransferHandler`); importing .md / .txt still makes TEXT notes. Help: eleven kinds of notes, and the formatted-text section describes the Word kind, in all ten languages. **Trademark note, told to the user:** "Word" is a Microsoft trademark; fine for private use, a risk for a public release (GitHub / F-Droid) — the label is one string per language and easy to change (e.g. "Document") before publication. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.34 |
| D213 | **One toolbar at a time in a text note; the blueprint gets the drawing's Shapes tool (user, 2026-09-23, after 0.9.32).** (1) The "Mode: Markdown / Formatted" row above the text is gone: the first button of the toolbar under the text ("MD ⇄" or "Aa ⇄") switches the note to the other way of writing and so to the other toolbar; only one toolbar is ever shown. View / Edit now belongs to the text note, not to each editor, so switching stays in Edit. (2) Blueprint: the free-hand **pen is removed** from the tools ("a horror"; old pen shapes still draw and can be edited); the separate shape chips are replaced by one **Shapes** tool with the same list as a drawing (12 primitives) and a **Filled** switch (the fill colour below, or none). The blueprint help text lists the new tools in all ten languages. `AppFlowTest.theBlueprintToolChipsFollowEveryTap` follows the new toolbar. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.33 |
| D212 | **Drawing and blueprint tools (user, 2026-09-23).** Drawing: the **brush** has opacity (10-100 %), softness (a blurred edge) and a **flat nib** at an adjustable angle like a calligraphy pen, or a round tip (`Stroke.opacity/softness/nibAngle`, the nib drawn as one path of the quadrilaterals it sweeps); **Shapes** (rectangle, square, ellipse, circle, triangle, rhombus, pentagon, hexagon, star, line, arrow, double arrow; filled or outline) dragged out on the sheet and painted in as a raster operation (`ShapeStamp`, undoable); **Text** (tap, then words, size, bold / italic / underline / strikethrough — the second text panel, no Markdown) painted in; the **Eyedropper** takes the colour of the sheet (a see-through pixel blended on white) and goes back to the tool before it. Blueprint: the same new primitives (vector, editable), **text shapes** (`ShapeType.TEXT`, `Shape.text/textSize/textStyle`, box measured when made; move them, "Edit text" when one is selected) and an **eyedropper** that takes the fill inside a filled shape, else its line colour (the tools that act by a tap drop the selection first, so nothing is recoloured by accident). Texts use the bundled Roboto (D209). Files of older versions open unchanged; older versions keep and re-save the new shape kinds without drawing them (D84). `DrawingToolsTest` (5); `VectorModelTest` now uses "TRAPEZOID" as its example of an unknown kind (HEXAGON exists now). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.32 |
| D211 | **Formatted text: a second way of writing a text note, switched per note (user, 2026-09-23; the user chose "a switch in the note" out of three designs).** Edit tab: "Mode: Markdown / Formatted". Formatted text (`domain/model/RichText.kt`: `RichDocument` = the text + non-overlapping styled runs + one `RichPara` per line) is stored as JSON in the note's `extras` (`{"format":"rich",...}`, the text itself only in `contentText`, so search, cards, backups, clones and TXT keep working). Toolbar under the text: bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, super / subscript, size (75-200 %), text colour, highlight, bullet / numbered / check lists, indent - / +, align left / centre / right, clear formatting; with nothing selected a button sets the style of what is typed next; Enter on an empty list item ends the list; the editor shows the markers, indents and alignment in place (a visual transformation with an offset mapping that steps over the markers). Markdown -> formatted keeps headings (bold + bigger), lists, quotes, bold / italic / strikethrough / links; formatted -> Markdown keeps bold, italic, strikethrough, headings (a whole line bold at 2x / 1.5x / 1.25x) and lists, and warns before dropping colours, sizes, underline, scripts, alignment and indents (Undo brings them back). Export of a formatted note (Markdown, TXT, PDF) goes through its Markdown form. Undo / Redo (D197) and the footer stamps (D198) work as for Markdown. A test caught a real bug before release: the `format` marker was not written (equal to its default); the stored class now has no defaults. `RichTextTest` (7). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.32 |
| D210 | **The Markdown toolbar moved under the text (user, 2026-09-23: "when text is selected the phone's copy / paste menu covers the Markdown bar").** The phone's own menu appears over the selected words, so a toolbar at the top was under it; the field now comes first and the toolbar sits under it, just above the keyboard (`imePadding`), in both text modes. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.32 |
| D209 | **The app has its own font: bold and italic did not show on the user's phone at all (user, 2026-09-23, after 0.9.30: "B and I do not change the font; lists and numbering do work"; "remember, we have many languages").** Everything in the code was right (the tests see the bold and italic spans); what failed is the phone's font: many makers (and BlueStacks, or a chosen "font style") give apps a system font without bold / italic faces, so Compose drew them like plain text. Lists and numbering need no font face, which is why they worked. Fix: Roboto (SIL Open Font License 1.1) is bundled — regular, medium, bold, italic, bold italic, `res/font/roboto_*.ttf`, ~1.9 MB — and every Material text style uses it (`core/ui/theme/Type.kt`, `AppFontFamily`), so headings, titles, the editor, the View and the cards all have real faces. Letters Roboto lacks (Chinese, Japanese, Korean, emoji) come from the phone's fonts letter by letter, as before; Latin and Cyrillic (Belarusian included) are in Roboto. The licence text ships in `assets/licenses/Roboto-OFL.txt` and is listed in `NOTICE.md`. Also: a quote in the View gets a bar on its left and italics (it was only a little greyer). APK 59.5 -> 60.6 MB. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.31 |
| D208 | **Markdown styles did not show well (user, 2026-09-23: "bold looks the same as plain text, italic too — check all the Markdown commands").** Four causes, all fixed: (1) Android selects a word WITH the space after it, and the B / I / S / code buttons made `**word **`, which Markdown does not treat as bold — spaces and line breaks at the ends of a selection now stay outside the markers; (2) a selection of several lines made one style across the line break, which never matches — each line is now styled on its own; (3) bold + italic `***x***` showed stray stars — it is now a style of its own; (4) weak look: bold is drawn extra-bold (800 instead of 700), inline code has a grey background. The same styles in the View, on cards and in the editor. Every toolbar command is checked from button to View in `MarkdownCommandsTest`. Also: the sorted rows of a list and a schedule are computed once per change, not on every frame (`remember`; `UnitOption` is a data class so the key compares). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.30 |
| D207 | **The crash in the search and while scrolling 40+ notes found: a web address (user, 2026-09-23, the stack trace from the D203 "black box").** `MarkdownRender.appendInline` made a bare `https://…` a link whose label was the same text, styled again by `appendInline`, which found the same address again: endless recursion, `StackOverflowError`, the app closed whenever a card or the View of a note with a web address was drawn — in the search results (D204's guess was wrong) and when such a card scrolled into view. Present since 0.9.24 (D191). Fix: inside a link nothing is searched for another link; a bare address is shown as typed. Defence: `inline`, `highlight` and `preview` show plain text if styling ever fails (any exception or a stack overflow), so no text can close the app again. `MarkdownFuzzTest` now mixes web addresses and link brackets into its 20 000 random texts (the old alphabet had no `http`, which is why it missed this). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.30 |
| D206 | **The View of a text note keeps the lines as typed (user, 2026-09-23: "five lines typed are shown as one line — not acceptable").** `MarkdownRender.blocks` followed Markdown's soft-break rule and joined consecutive plain lines with a space; now each typed line stays a line (the lines of a paragraph are joined with a line break, inline styles never cross a line), and every empty line between texts is kept as an empty line (none before the first text or after the last). Checked the other kinds: list and schedule show names and comments with their line breaks already; names are typed on one line; a budget row shows the first 16 characters of its comment on purpose (D177). Card previews were never affected (they take the lines one by one). Test: `MarkdownRenderTest` (the old test that expected the joining was changed). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.29 |
| D205 | **Documentation and process (user, 2026-09-22).** (1) **`Notio-iOS` is dropped**: the user removed it; rule 13 and §2.1 no longer mention it, and the Windows/Linux docs no longer point to it. (2) **Rule 13 rewritten**: `Notio-Windows/AGENT.md` and `Notio-Linux/AGENT.md` each get a §5 "Feature parity with Android" table (one row per user-facing Android decision, Android version, `PLANNED`), updated in the same working step as any new user-facing decision here; both brought in step up to D205 now. (3) **Translation sources back in step**: `translations/src/*.tsv` for be/es/it/zh/ja/ko lacked 61 keys that had been put straight into `strings.xml` (PIN pad and database encryption, D185-D192), so `gen_lang.py` refused to run. New script `translations/sync_tsv_from_xml.py <tag>` copies what is in the built `strings.xml` but in no tsv file into `<tag>.part_synced.tsv`; after it, `gen_lang.py` gives back the same `strings.xml` (checked: no change except line endings). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D204 | **The app closed as soon as a word was typed into the search, with 100+ notes (user, 2026-09-22).** Not reproduced: a Robolectric flow with 130 notes of all ten kinds in four folders, typing seven different searches, does not fail, and 20 000 random Markdown lines never make the card previews throw (`MarkdownFuzzTest`). No stack trace was available. What only the search does: it puts a **focused text field inside the lazy list** of the main screen (item 2, after the folder strip) while every typed letter rebuilds the rest of that list — a known source of crashes with a real keyboard (bring-into-view and pinning of the focused item). Fix: the search panel is no longer an item of the list, it stands **above** it (`HomeScreen`, a `Column` with the panel and the `LazyColumn`), which also keeps it in view while the results scroll; the tag chips of the panel scroll within 132 dp so many tags cannot push the results off the screen. D203 records the error if it still happens. **Needs the phone to confirm.** | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28, not confirmed |
| D203 | **"Black box" for crashes (assistant, 2026-09-22, prompted by D204).** `core/common/CrashLog`: an uncaught-exception handler (installed first thing in `NoteBudgetApplication.onCreate`, then handing over to Android's own) writes the version, the Android version, the phone model, the thread and the stack trace (at most 20 000 characters) to `files/last_error.txt`; the next start shows `LastErrorDialog` (after the lock, never over it) with the text, **Copy error text** and **Close** (Close deletes the file). Only the error is kept, no note content; nothing leaves the phone by itself. Same idea as the encryption diagnostics of D189 that found that bug in one round. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D202 | **UI tests of the dialogs where text is typed (user, 2026-09-22; closes the old "dialogs with text input are not covered" gap).** Investigated first: under Robolectric **any dialog window holding a text field never becomes idle** — AlertDialog, BasicAlertDialog and a plain `Dialog` all hang ("Compose did not get idle"), with the clock advanced by hand or automatically, with or without a label, with `decorFitsSystemWindows` false; the same AlertDialog without the field is idle in 9 s. The app's dialogs with text input now go through `core/ui/AppAlertDialog`, which **is** Material's AlertDialog in the app and draws the same content in place when a test provides `LocalInlineDialogs = true`. `DialogInputTest` (7 tests) types into the real New note, New folder, Change (+ / − / = with its errors), budget item (name, sum, goal, comment; numbers read in minor units) and Comment dialogs. Converted so far: `AdjustDialog`, `HomeDialogs` (all), `CommentDialog`, the budget dialogs, the list item dialogs; the other dialogs can move over when a test needs them. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D201 | **Empty states and error messages, every screen checked (user, 2026-09-22).** Lists with an empty text already: main screen (no notes, nothing found), trash, reminders, tags, units, currencies, budget log, audio / photo / video / screen notes, lists, schedules, budgets. One gap fixed: a **folder** search that finds nothing left the screen blank; it now says "Nothing found" like the note search. Failures of export, import and recording already end in a message (checked the `runCatching` blocks of those screens). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D200 | **Performance pass (user, 2026-09-22: "find what is not optimal, make it faster without losing quality or reliability").** (1) The main screen's filtering, search and sorting of all notes ran on the **main thread** after every change, including every autosave of an open note: `HomeViewModel` now takes a `computeDispatcher` and the app passes `Dispatchers.Default` (tests keep the old synchronous path). (2) Sorting worked a key out in **every comparison** (a colour parsed, a date turned into a day, a name lower-cased ~2·n·log n times): keys are now computed once per item. (3) The featured note of each folder was found by a scan of all notes per folder: one map. (4) Each note card searched the folder list for its folder's colour: one map, remembered. (5) Card pictures of drawings and media were read from disk and decoded again every time a card scrolled back into view: `ThumbnailCache` (an LRU of at most 1/8 of the app's memory, keys carry the note's or file's time so a changed picture is never stale). (6) Audio and media lists found each row's number with `indexOf` (quadratic): `itemsIndexed`. (7) The Markdown highlight of the text editor re-styled the whole text on every recomposition, cursor moves included: the last result is kept. (8) Start-up read three settings in three blocking waits: one. Not done (bigger, listed in `TODO_IDEAS_RU.md`): a card-only projection of notes without their full text; measuring on a phone with thousands of notes. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D199 | **"Not allowed" shown in red although the switch was on (user, 2026-09-22, about the alarm over the lock screen on Android 14).** The test alarm worked, but Settings -> Security -> Reminder alarm still said in red that the phone does not let Notio open the alarm over the lock screen. Cause: the permission was read once, when the page opened, and not again after the person switched it on in the phone's settings and came back. `core/ui/rememberResumeCount()` counts returns to the screen; the alarm page, the exact-alarm hint of the reminders screen and the floating-button hint of screen recordings now read their permission again on every return. So the message was a stale warning, not a real refusal. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D198 | **Stamps at the bottom of a note, for each kind on its own (user, 2026-09-22; the idea recorded under D195).** Settings -> Appearance -> **Stamps at the bottom of a note**: a chip per kind of note (all ten; a dot marks the kinds with something on) and, for the chosen kind, two switches: "created" and "changed". The View of a note of that kind shows them **after all its content** (text, list, budget, schedule, audio and media lists: the last row; drawing and blueprint: under the sheet), in the global date-time format (D138). Off for every kind by default. The stamps at the top of a note and on cards (D157) are unchanged and separate. `NoteFooterStamps` (JSON of kind ids) in the settings and in backups (`SettingsRecord.footerStamps`); the note screen provides `LocalNoteFooter`, every editor calls `NoteFooter()`; the changed time follows what the editors save (the screen observes the note). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D197 | **Undo / Redo in every editable note (user, 2026-09-22).** Text, list, budget, drawing, blueprint and schedule notes get two arrows in the **top bar** of the note (Undo, Redo; greyed when there is nothing). The number of steps is **Settings -> Notes and export -> Undo steps**: 1, 3, 5, 10, 20 or 50, **3 by default**, in backups (`SettingsRecord.undoSteps`); the history lives while the note is open. Audio, photo, video and screen notes: none (as the user said). **How:** `core/common/UndoHistory` (two stacks, a limit, a new change drops the redo). Text / list / schedule: `NoteEditorViewModel` keeps snapshots of the text and the structure; changes less than 1 s apart are one step (a word typed), the first change after an undo is always a step of its own; the schedule's fields follow a restored value. Budget: `BudgetViewModel` snapshots the whole tree before each data change (add, edit, Change button, move, delete, drag — not opening / closing a group) and `BudgetEditor.restoreNodes` writes a snapshot back in one transaction (parents before children, rows swept away by a cascade written again, open / closed kept as it is now) and logs "Last change undone" / "Undone change redone" (`BudgetLogKind.UNDONE` / `REDONE`). Drawing: raster and vector layers had Undo (50 steps) in their toolbars; they now have Redo too, follow the setting, and the arrows moved to the top bar (the toolbar buttons were at the end of a scrolling row, often off screen). **Changed on purpose:** a drawing now keeps 3 steps by default instead of 50 — raise it in Settings. Shared plumbing: `UndoTarget`, `LocalUndoSlot`, `ProvideUndoTarget`, `UndoRedoButtons` (own two vector icons, no extended icon library). | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |
| D196 | **Group headers when sorted by colour (user, 2026-09-22: "for folders and for notes").** Sorted by colour, the notes get a header over each colour group and the folder strip a label in front of each group: a dot in the group's first colour and the family name — reds, oranges, yellows, greens, light blues, blues, purples, pinks, "greys, black and white", "no colour of its own". Pinned notes and folders stay on top without a header; no headers while a finger drags or while searching. `Sorting.colorFamily`, `colorGroupStarts`; the colour rank now puts the reds just below 360 degrees in front of the reds at 0, so one family is never split between the start and the end. | ACCEPTED, done in 0.9.28 |

Recorded in `TODO_IDEAS_RU.md` as a small housekeeping item; does not affect what ships. | ACCEPTED. **STATUS: DONE in 0.9.27, compiles, 694 tests pass, `LocalizationTest` passes for all ten languages (this was actually run, not assumed — see the TSV-drift note above for why that was worth double-checking this time).
