### 21.4a Verification build 0.8.0 (versionCode 11) - what to check

File: `dist/Notio-by-vibic777-0.8.0-debug.apk` (49.7 MB, 49727049 bytes, SHA-256 `0b6607e47d4fb4d84c14875ac03cbdd770f86137fa1d85b23241a7994232eaa4`), debug-signed with the same key, so it installs over 0.7.0 and keeps the data (database v5 -> v6 by migration). Settings -> Backup and restore -> About must say version 0.8.0 (build 11), database version 6. **Take a full backup first.** The items are ordered by risk, the riskiest first:

1. **Screen recording.** New note -> Screen recording -> Record screen: allow the notifications, the microphone (if a sound with the microphone is chosen) and the screen capture; the app steps aside; record something, then stop with the Stop button of the notification; open the note: an item with a small picture, its length and its stamps must be there and must play. Repeat with the sound: none, microphone, the phone's sound (play music), both. Repeat with "Display over other apps" allowed: the red button must appear, be draggable and stop the recording; note whether it shows in the video (on Android 15+ it should not).
2. **Camera video.** New note -> Camera video: allow the camera; try the back, the front and any other camera in the list; record with no sound, the microphone, the phone's sound, both; stop; the item must play with the sound.
3. **Photo.** New note -> Photo: take several photos in a row; each appears in the list with its small picture; open full screen, swipe, pinch to zoom, turn the phone (portrait and landscape).
4. **Overlays.** In each of the three notes: Overlays: switch on the time stamp (choose formats, colour, size, position), the place (needs the location permission), a text and a picture (choose a logo; try a plate and a transparent background); the preview must match the result on a new photo / video / screen recording; the time stamp on a video must run.
5. **Drag and drop.** Main screen: take a folder card and a note card by the four-arrow grip and move them; the list must scroll at the edge; "My order" must be chosen afterwards and the order must survive a restart. Pin a note and a folder: the pinned ones stay first, the last pinned on top; pinning must not change "Changed".
6. **Sorting.** Sort dialog: try every key ascending and descending (name, tags, colour, kind, created / changed as a moment, as a day, as a time of day; folders also by number of notes).
7. **Settings.** Settings is a menu of pages; the search field must find "trash", "password", "font" ...; the trash's "Trash settings" button must land on the Trash page; the trash setting must be "never" on a fresh install.
8. **Backup password.** Settings -> Backup and restore: switch on, type a password, Save, look at it with Show, export: the file ends in `.enc`; import it back (the saved password is tried first); try a wrong password (nothing must change); switch protection off and export a plain backup.
9. **Dates and names.** Settings -> Language and formats: change the date and time format: cards, notes, clips and media items must follow. Notes and export: switch on a date and a time stamp, export a note and an audio clip and look at the file names.
10. **Selection mode.** In an audio note and a photo note: Select, tick some, Select all, Clear selection, Delete; the files must be gone.
11. **Zoom and tabs.** Text, list and budget: View first, then Edit, the zoom buttons at the right of the tabs; a drawing on a tablet: the zoom must not cover the sheet.
12. **Folder colours.** New / edit folder: the colour row must offer the ready colours, the recent ones and RGB.
13. **Show buttons.** Every password field (lock, recovery, mail, backup) must have Show / Hide.
14. **Audio place.** Settings -> Notes and export -> Place of audio recordings on; record; the place must show under the clip.
15. **Turn and sleep.** Turn the phone during a camera video (it must not stop) and during the note lists; lock the screen during a screen recording.

Items 14 - 21 of section 21.4 (0.7.0) and the older ones are still open too.

### 21.4b Verification builds 0.8.1 - 0.9.7 (versionCode 12 - 24) - what to check

File: `dist/Notio-by-vibic777-0.9.7-debug.apk` (50383461 bytes, SHA-256 `375144cfb93b6b0dd45c95e2713072813a422454ef1a3304d5cdbd22190808f4`), debug-signed with the same key: it installs over the earlier ones and keeps the data (database up to v8 by migrations). Settings -> About must say version 0.9.7 (build 24), database version 8. **Take a full backup first.** Nothing below has been run on a device.

16. **Alarm on the lock screen (Android 14).** Settings -> Security -> Reminder alarm: check both permission lines, press **Test the alarm in 10 seconds**, lock the phone: the alarm screen must come over the lock screen with two buttons only (Repeat in N min / Turn off, or Done). After **Turn off** the notification must be gone; while any reminder is set, the quiet notification "Reminders are on" stays in the shade (switchable).
17. **Snooze.** Snooze from the notification and from the alarm screen; it rings again on time, also after a restart of the phone; the reminder list says "Snoozed until ...".
18. **Schedule note.** New note -> Schedule: add times, add a calculator (two times: the difference "1 h 30 min"; 23:00 -> 01:00 = 2 h; a deleted time gives "?"); drag rows by the grip in View **and** in Edit; export JSON / TXT / PDF.
19. **Dragging in lists.** An ordinary list note: the grip on every row, in View and in Edit.
20. **Media trash.** In an audio, a photo, a video and a screen note delete one item and several with check marks: they go to the trash (Settings -> Trash: days), where **Restore** (same note, same place), **Export** and **Delete for good** work; a note in the trash: restore the note first; a note deleted for good: only Export. Take a backup, import it on the same phone: the trash list is back and restorable.
21. **Cards.** Settings -> Appearance: preview 0 / 1 / 2 lines (names of the recordings for audio and media), pictures on cards (none / first / deck of three; drawings on / off; a drawing lies on white paper), four time-stamp switches. The sort of the notes is per folder ("Apply to all folders" box).
22. **Folder card and strip.** The card: three buttons on top (select, grip, menu), then icon and name, count, stamps. The app opens on the folder where you left it; the strip of folders must **not jump to the last folder** (tell me when it does: opening, returning from a note, adding a folder). New folder / New note also in the ⋮ menu. Dragging folders by the grip scrolls the strip at the edge.
23. **Blueprint fill.** Blueprint -> fill a shape, leave the note, come back: the fill must be there, and on the card preview too.
24. **Budget "Change" button (0.9.7).** Budget note: on every **item** (not on a group) there is a +/- icon, in View and in Edit. Tap it: choose + or -, type an amount (dot or comma), **Save**: the sum of the item changes at once and the group and total sums follow; **Cancel** changes nothing; a zero or empty amount is refused; the result may be negative. An item with quantity 3: the change applies to the sum of one piece (the dialog says so). Tell me whether you would rather change the goal too, or the total of an item with a quantity.

25. **Change with "Enter" and the list (0.9.8).** Budget item -> Change -> **= Enter**: the number typed replaces the sum. List note: **Edit** -> Add item (dialog: name, quantity and unit, in the "Prices" mode the price and its unit, comment) and Add unit total; tap a card to edit it; the ⋮ menu has Edit and Delete. In **View and in Edit** every item has **Quantity** (and, in the "Prices" mode, **Price**) buttons: + Add, − Subtract, = Enter, Save / Cancel; the number changes at once and the cost and the totals follow; subtracting more than there is must be refused; drag the rows by the grip, tick the check boxes in View. Old lists must open unchanged. Report anything that feels worse than the old inline fields.

26. **Log of the budget (0.9.9).** Budget note: change things (add items and groups, edit an item's sum / goal / quantity, use Change with +, − and =, rename, move an item to another group, delete). Press **Log of changes** (in View and in Edit): every step must be there, newest first, with the time and readable words; nothing is written for an edit that changed nothing. ⋮ -> **Save as TXT** and **Save as PDF**: pick a place, open the file (the PDF has never been checked for this text; Cyrillic must print). ⋮ -> **Clear the log**: it asks, then the log is empty and the budget is unchanged. Make a backup, restore it: the log must come back. Delete the note for good: its log goes.
27. **Select in the folder menu (0.9.9).** Folder ⋮ -> **Select**: the folder opens, the notes get check boxes, the bar at the bottom shows the count; tick some, **Select all**, **Clear selection**, **Delete (N)** -> the confirmation -> the notes go to the trash and the snackbar offers **Undo**; **Done** ends the mode; opening another folder ends it too. Also try Select on a folder that is not open.
28. **Time stamps (0.9.9).** On a fresh install the cards show no created / changed lines (notes and folders); Settings -> Appearance -> Time stamps switches them on. An install that had them on keeps them.

29. **Reminder bell (0.9.10).** Give a note a reminder (⋮ -> Reminders): a small bell must appear beside its name on the card; cancel or delete the reminder (or let a one-time one ring and confirm it): the bell goes; a recurring or snoozed reminder keeps it. Nothing but the bell is shown.

30. **Currency of a budget (0.9.11).** Settings -> Notes and export -> **Currencies**: the built-in list (sign - name in your language), add your own ("USDT"), a duplicate or a 9-character name is refused. Budget note -> **Currency: none** button (View and Edit): pick ₽ / your own / none; every sum (rows, the Change dialog, the card of the note, the log, the log's TXT / PDF, the text export) must carry the sign after it. A currency a budget uses cannot be deleted from the list (the message says so); change the budget's currency and it can. Backup and restore: the custom currencies and the choice of each budget come back.

31. **Streamed backups (0.9.13).** Settings -> Backup: export a backup of a phone with many photos, videos and recordings (ideally a few GB): the waiting dialog appears with the warning and a file counter, the app stays alive, the file is saved; note the time. Import it (Replace all) on the same phone or another: the counter runs, everything comes back (open a video and a photo). Try a protected backup too. A file over 2 GB or a backup over 3.5 GB: the toast says how many files were left out. Report the time and whether the phone got hot or the app closed.
32. **Budget header (0.9.13).** In **Edit**: "Display options" alone on the first line, then "Log of changes" and "Currency: ..." together; in **View**: the Log and the Currency. On a narrow screen (or a big font) the currency button moves to the line below the log. Tap the currency: the list opens.

33. **Dragging in a budget (0.9.14).** Budget note with several groups and items: take a group by its grip (four arrows) and drag it above / below another group; open the group afterwards: everything inside came along; the sums are unchanged. Drag an item inside a group; try in View and in Edit, with a long budget (the list scrolls at the edge) and with "Newest items first" switched on in Display options. While you hold a group the other groups of its level look closed: check that the row stays under the finger (tell me if it jumps). Leave the note and come back: the order stayed.

34. **Comments (0.9.15).** Note card ⋮ -> **Comment...**: type a long comment, save: the card shows the first 16 characters (last one "…") as its last line; same for a folder card (⋮ -> Comment...). Settings -> Appearance -> **Comments on cards**: switch notes and folders off and on. Budget -> Edit -> **Display options** -> "Show comments": rows show 16 characters or none; open the edit dialog of an item: the whole comment is there. Old notes open normally after the update (the database changes to v10). Backup and restore: comments come back.

35. **Sorting in a budget and in a list (0.9.16).** Budget: the button **Sort: My order** next to the Log and the Currency; choose Name, Created, Changed, Sum, Goal, Remainder, Progress, each ascending and descending: the rows of every group are sorted and the grips disappear; "My order" brings the dragged order back. List (View and Edit): the same button; try Name, Created (rows added before this update have no date and come last), Quantity, Unit, Done / not done; in the "Prices" mode also Price and Cost; rows without a value are last both ways; "Total by unit" rows stay after the items. Leave and come back: the sort stayed.

36. **Cloning (0.9.17).** Note card ⋮ -> **Clone** for a text note, a list, a budget, a drawing, a blueprint, an audio note, a photo / video / screen note and a schedule: a snackbar says "Clone created: name_clone1" and the clone is in the same folder with the same content (open it: budget rows, drawing, recordings, photos, videos play). Clone again: `_clone2`; clone the clone: `_clone3`. Change the clone: the original stays as it was. The clone has the tags and the comment of the original but no pin, star or reminder. Try a note with a big video: how long it takes, and that the app stays alive.

37. **Pin instead of the star (0.9.18).** A note that had a star before the update is now pinned (📌) and stays near the top; the card menu has Pin / Unpin and no favourites; the last pinned note is the top one; unpin puts it back. Restore an old backup: its starred notes come in pinned.
38. **Backgrounds (0.9.18).** Settings -> Appearance -> Choose backgrounds: the list must now have a row for every kind of note: text, list, budget, drawing, blueprint, audio, **schedule, photo, camera video, screen recording**. Give one a colour and open a note of that kind: the page has the colour (the video player of a photo / video note keeps its dark box).
39. **Sorting a schedule (0.9.18).** Schedule note: **Sort: My order** button; choose Time and Name (both directions): the items are sorted, the calculators stay after them, the grips are hidden; My order brings the dragged order back.
40. **Collapse / expand all (0.9.18).** Budget with nested groups: **Collapse all groups** shows only the top-level groups (closed); **Expand all groups** opens everything; the total stays open.
41. **Database encryption (0.9.19-0.9.20, D184, D186) — the riskiest item in this build, take a full backup first (Settings -> Export all data).** Settings -> Data -> **Database encryption**: turn the switch on — a confirmation dialog, then a short "Converting..." dialog, then a generated password appears with Show/Hide and Copy; tap **Restart now**. After the restart every note, folder and budget row must still be there. Turn it off the same way: confirm, convert, restart, check the data again. If it says "Could not encrypt the database" (D186, seen once already on a real phone, mitigated with retries but not confirmed fixed): note the small red diagnostic line under the message and report it exactly, then try the switch again (retries built in now). **Send password by e-mail** (needs SMTP recovery already set up in Settings -> App lock -> Recovery): check it actually arrives, and that the button explains itself when SMTP recovery is not set up yet. **Database file:** export while encrypted, then **Import database file** the same file back and confirm the password prompt appears, the saved password is tried first, a deliberately wrong password is refused without changing anything, and the right one asks to restart, then works; also try exporting/importing with encryption off (a plain file, no password asked), and importing a plain file over an encrypted database and back. Try force-closing the app during "Converting..." if you can catch the timing, then relaunch: the original data must still open (nothing should be left half-converted).
42. **App lock: PIN code, vertical method list, configurable wrong-try policy (0.9.20, D185) — also take a full backup first if you turn on the erase option.** Settings -> App lock: the "Change password/method" dialog's method list must now show **one chip per line** (PIN, Pattern, Password, Login and password) — this is also the bug fix (the third option used to be pushed off screen). Turn the lock on with a **PIN** (digits only, 4-8 long, numeric keypad) and confirm it actually unlocks the app. Scroll to **"Wrong-try policy"**: change "Free tries" and "First wait (seconds)", Save, then get the secret wrong enough times to see the new wait value. Set **"Limit (0 = off)"** to something small like 3, choose **"Block guessing, recovery only"**, Save: fail 3 times in a row and confirm direct guessing (and Change/Turn off) is refused and only "I forgot it" gets back in; recovering must clear the lockout. **Only if you are prepared to lose the phone's data to test it properly:** set the limit's mode to **"Erase the app's data"** — confirm the typed-phrase dialog refuses a wrong phrase and only accepts the exact word, then (on a phone with nothing you need, or right after a fresh backup) actually reach the limit and confirm the app's data is genuinely gone and the process restarts cleanly, not stuck half-erased.

43. **Undo / Redo (0.9.28, D197).** In a text note type a word, pause, type another: the Undo arrow (top bar) takes away the second word, again the first; Redo brings both back. List: tick an item, change a quantity -> Undo twice. Budget: add an item, use Change +50 -> Undo twice: the total goes back each time; the Log shows "Last change undone". Drawing and blueprint: draw two strokes / shapes -> Undo, Redo (the arrows are now in the top bar, not in the tools row). Schedule: edit a description -> Undo shows the old text in the field. Settings -> Notes and export -> **Undo steps**: set 1 and check only one step goes back. Audio / photo / video / screen notes have no arrows.
44. **Stamps at the bottom (0.9.28, D198).** Settings -> Appearance -> **Stamps at the bottom of a note**: pick Text, switch on both: a text note's View ends with "Created ... / Changed ..."; other kinds show nothing until switched on for them; the budget's "Changed" follows a new change.
45. **Colour groups (0.9.28, D196).** Give notes several colours, sort notes by colour: a header (dot + "Reds", "Greens" ...) above each group; pinned notes stay on top without a header. Same for folders (sort folders by colour): a label in front of each group in the strip.
46. **Alarm permission text (0.9.28, D199).** Settings -> Security -> Reminder alarm: if it says "NOT allowed" in red, allow it in the phone's settings and come back: the line must turn to "allowed" at once.
47. **Search (0.9.28, D204) — the crash you reported.** With your 100+ notes open the search and type: it must not close. The search panel is now above the list and stays while results scroll. **If the app still closes**, open it again: a window "The app closed with an error" appears — press **Copy error text** and send it.
48. **Speed (0.9.28, D200).** Scroll the notes of a big folder up and down: drawing and photo pictures on the cards should appear at once when they come back into view; typing in a note and switching folders should feel no slower than before.

49. **Line breaks in the View of a text (0.9.29, D206).** Type five short lines, an empty line and two more lines in a text note; View shows them exactly so, each on its own line, the empty line too. Bold / italic on a line still work.
50. **Web addresses (0.9.30, D207).** A note with `https://…` in its text: open the search and type, scroll the notes past it, open it: no crash; the address is underlined and opens the browser.
51. **Markdown (0.9.30, D208).** In a text note select a word by a long press (it takes the space after it) and press B: the View shows it clearly bold; the same with I, S and </>; select two lines and press B: both bold; B then I on one word: bold italic without stars.
52. **Font (0.9.31, D209).** In a text note: **bold**, *italic*, ***both***, ~~strike~~, `code`, a quote (`> `), headings: in View each looks clearly different from plain text. Switch the app to Chinese / Japanese / Korean for a moment: the letters still show. The app's look elsewhere is almost unchanged (Roboto is Android's standard font).
53. **Toolbar (0.9.32, D210).** Edit a text note, select a word: the copy / paste menu appears, the toolbar under the text stays visible above the keyboard.
54. **Formatted text (0.9.32, D211).** Edit -> Mode: Formatted. Try every button on a selection and with nothing selected (then type): B, I, U, S, x², x₂, size, colours, lists (Enter continues a list, Enter on an empty item ends it), indents, alignments, clear. View shows the same; ticks in a check list work in View. Switch back to Markdown: a warning when colours / sizes / alignment would be lost. Export to PDF. Undo / Redo. Close and reopen the note: the formatting is still there.
55. **Drawing tools (0.9.32, D212).** Brush: opacity, softness, flat nib at several angles. Shapes: each of the 12, filled and not; square and circle stay equal-sided. Text: size and styles, Cyrillic and a CJK word. Eyedropper on a colour and on empty paper (white). Undo each. Blueprint: the new shapes, a text (move it, Edit text), the eyedropper on a filled and an outline shape.
56. **One toolbar (0.9.33, D213).** Text note, Edit: no "Mode" row at the top; the first toolbar button "MD ⇄" switches to formatted text and the toolbar changes ("Aa ⇄" back), staying in Edit.
57. **Blueprint shapes (0.9.33, D213).** Edit a blueprint: no Pen; Shapes shows the 12 primitives and Filled; draw each filled and not.
58. **Word notes (0.9.34, D214).** New note -> the list has "Word"; it opens with the formatted toolbar and no switch; Text notes have only the Markdown toolbar. A text note you made formatted in 0.9.32 / 0.9.33 is now a Word note with its formatting. Settings -> Backgrounds has "Word notes"; Stamps at the bottom has a Word tab. Export a Word note to PDF.
59. **Change in a budget (0.9.35, D215).** The buttons show only + − =. Sum is on, Goal off; try switching Sum off (refused while Goal is off); Goal on + Sum on, −1000: both drop by 1000, "Will be" shows both; Goal only, = 5000. The log shows the lines; Undo takes it back. A list's Change dialog shows + − = too.
60. **Share (0.9.35, D216).** Card ⋮ -> Share… for a text, a Word, a list, a budget, a schedule (text arrives in Telegram / WhatsApp), a drawing (a picture), a photo note with several photos (all of them), an audio note, an empty photo note ("nothing to share").
61. ~~REMOVED in 0.9.40 (D223).~~ **Cloud, stage 0 (D217).** With Google Drive / Yandex Disk installed: Settings -> Data -> Export all data -> choose Drive / Yandex Disk in the picker; then Import all data from there. Tell me whether it worked — it decides the cloud plan.
62. ~~REMOVED in 0.9.40 (D223).~~ **Cloud backups (0.9.36, D218).** Set a backup password first. Settings -> Backup and restore -> Cloud backups -> Google Drive: Choose a folder (open Drive in the picker; if Drive offers no folders, Choose a file). Back up now: "Backup saved", the status line shows time and size, the file appears in Drive. Automatically: Once a day. Restore: the import asks the mode. The same for Yandex Disk. Without a password: buttons off, red hint.
63. **Colour themes (0.9.37, D219).** Settings -> Appearance -> Colour theme: Yellow, Lilac, Magenta in light and dark (switch the system dark mode); Your colour -> RGB… -> pick a very pale, a very dark and a grey colour: every screen stays readable. After a backup and a restore on another install the picked colour comes back.
64. ~~REMOVED in 0.9.40 (D223).~~ **Cloud through the account (0.9.38, D220).** Needs the setup of `docs/CLOUD_SETUP_RU.md` first (Google: Android OAuth client with the debug SHA-1, your account in Test users; Yandex: `yandex.clientId` in local.properties and a rebuild). Settings -> Backup and restore -> Cloud backups -> Google Drive -> Sign in with Google: the phone's account chooser, then "Account: you@gmail.com". Back up now -> a file in "Notio backups" on drive.google.com. Keep copies 2, back up three times: two stay. Restore -> choose one -> the import dialog. Test connection: all ✓. Same for Yandex Disk ("Applications/Notio"). Turn off Wi-Fi and mobile data -> Test connection: ✗ on Internet. Sign out -> the sign-in button is back.
65. **Error window (0.9.40, D224).** Cannot be forced from the menu; if Notio ever closes with an error: a window over the screen right away, "Notio closed because of an error", Copy error text -> paste it into a chat: the text is there; Open Notio again opens the app; Close closes the window and the next start shows nothing. Settings -> Backup and restore: no Cloud backups block any more; Help -> Backup has no cloud sentence.
66. **Sync between devices (0.9.41, D226).** Two phones (or a phone and a BlueStacks / emulator on the same Wi-Fi), both on Settings -> Sync. On A: Wait for a device -> a code. On B: Find a device -> A is in the list (or type the address shown on A) -> type the code -> A asks "Connect with B?" -> Allow: both show the connection. A wrong code: "wrong code"; three wrong ones: A shows a new code. On B: Sync… -> Both ways, Merge, All folders -> A asks, Allow -> both see "Synced", notes of each are on the other. Change a note on A, sync again: B gets the newer text. Sync… with chosen folders (check boxes; "Get the folders of A" lists A's). Replace is greyed out for both ways / chosen folders. Test connection: all ✓; switch Wi-Fi off on A: ✗ at the step that fails. Leave the page on A: B shows "Not seen now". Remove on B: removed on both. Sync log: lines, Copy.
106. **The last step is kept (0.9.82, D294).** A drawing: draw a line, at once Back; open it again: the line is there. Several lines fast, Back right after the last one: all there. The same in a blueprint (a shape, a text). A note, a list, a schedule: type, at once Back: all there; type and switch to another app straight away, then come back: all there.
105. **Reminder and the phone's alarm clock (0.9.80, D293).** A reminder with its own volume (below 100 %) or a fade-in, and the phone's alarm clock set to the same minute: the alarm clock rings -> the reminder's melody waits; turn the alarm clock off -> the melody goes on (at most a minute in all). The notification of the reminder: **Stop** first -> silence and no vibration, the reminder still waits for Done / Repeat. Open Reminders while it rings: at the top a red blinking light, "Ringing now: …" and a crossed-speaker button -> silence; the card of that reminder has the same light and button.
104. **R8 release, lock wait, themed icon (0.9.79, D289 - D292).** Install the **release** APK (about 19 MB) over the old one: it opens with all notes; go through every kind of note, a photo, a recording, the screen recording, a backup export and import, sync with the other phone, the lock (set, unlock, change), reminders and the merge of a budget at its time, Settings -> Licences; any crash window: Copy and send the text (class names are readable). Lock: 6 wrong tries -> a wait; move the phone's time an hour forward -> still waiting; back to the right time, wait it out -> it opens. Android 13+: Home screen -> Wallpaper & style -> Themed icons on: Notio shows a tinted sheet with lines.
103. **Back twice, big backups, lock rounds (0.9.78, D285 - D288).** Open a note, tap its arrow and at once the same corner again (where "Notio" shows): the main screen stays, no grey window; tap a note twice fast: it opens once, Back goes to the main screen. Unlock with a lock set before 0.9.78: it opens (the hash is renewed silently); lock and unlock again. Settings -> Data -> import a backup: as before. Sync while the phone is nearly full but has a large cache: it is not refused.
102. **Lock and sync storage (0.9.77, D283, D284).** Lock with a password: type a wrong one and tap OK five times fast — the screen does not freeze, every tap counts (the wait comes as many tries earlier as set). Sync: fill the receiving phone to less than 200 MB free + the size of the data — Sync says there is not enough free storage on the receiving device; nothing half-imported; free space and sync again — it works.
101. **Panel sides, sync address, log undo (0.9.76, D280 - D282).** A drawing → Edit → the dock icon → Tools on the left: the panel on the left, the sheet beside it; top and right too; leave and come back — the side is kept. Sync: a kept connection shows its address; Address… → type the other phone's new IP shown at the top of its Sync page → Sync works without pairing again; type a wrong IP → "not found"; another phone's IP → refused. A budget: +500 by mistake, then +3; Log → Undo on the +500 line → asked → the sum is +3 only, the line says "Undone"; undo an added item → asked (red) → gone. An older line has no Undo.
100. **Icon buttons (0.9.75, D278).** Press and hold each new icon: its name shows. Budget: clock → log, sliders (Edit) → display options, arrows → all groups closed / open; the currency and the sort are still words. Audio: checklist → selecting; the round ▶ plays and turns into ⏸. Selection: select all, clear, bin (asks). Trash: arrow out of the bin restores, bin with a cross asks and deletes. Reminder card: tick, pencil, crossed bell (asks), bin (asks). Tags / units / currencies: the pencil renames. List: list-plus and Σ; schedule: clock-plus, calculator, bubble. Blueprint: shape and layer icons. Dialog buttons and Empty trash still have words.
99. **Icon tabs and renaming (0.9.74, D276, D277).** Any note: the book and the pencil beside the zoom, the whole row fits; a long press shows "Просмотр" / "Правка"; with TalkBack they are read as tabs. Settings → Units: add "пачка", Rename → "упаковка", a list using it shows "упаковка". Settings → Currencies: add "USDT", choose it in a budget, Rename → "USDC": the budget shows "USDC"; renaming to "₽" or to another added one is refused with the reason.
98. **Ready folder name (0.9.73, D275).** Folders "Папка 1", "Папка 2", "Папка 4": New folder — the name is "Папка 3", Create at once; again — "Папка 5". Choose "Папка задач" — "Задачи 1"; type "Работа", switch the kind — the name stays "Работа".
97. **Zoom, tags, budget privacy, card colour (0.9.72, D270 - D273).** A text note on a phone in Russian: − 100% + all visible beside Просмотр / Правка. Settings → Tags → Add tag: "Work" adds it (0 notes); "work" again says it exists. A budget → Edit → Display → Hide sums: the card shows ***** for the sum, the note too, the remainder too; the eye beside the tabs shows them, leaving and opening the note hides them again; Hide goals alone hides goals and the remainder, not the sums; the log masks the same; Share still has the numbers. Settings → Appearance → Card colour: move to 80 % — cards strong, dark colours get light text; 10 % — pale as before.
96. **Reminders and tasks (0.9.71, D266 - D269).** With a task folder holding two tasks (one Done, one with an end yesterday) and one reminder: pull the shade — "Active: 1 · next: … · Tasks: 2 · open: 1 · overdue: 1"; delete the reminder — the notification stays titled "Tasks". Let a reminder ring and tap its notification: the calendar opens on today, the reminder framed; tap it — the note opens. Reminders: switch one off — paler, "Switched off", no bell on the note, the shade count drops; switch it on — back; a one-time one past its time says the time is in the past. Cancel — a dialog; Keep leaves it.
95. **Sheet background and Enter (0.9.70, D265, bug fix).** A drawing → Edit → the half-filled square icon: pick a colour → Save: the sheet takes it; tick Transparent, 100 %: a checkerboard; 50 %: the colour over the checkerboard. The bucket on the empty sheet fills it; the eraser shows the background again. ⋮ → Share into Telegram: the picture arrives on the chosen background (white for an old drawing, never black). A text note → Edit: type a word with suggestions showing and press Enter at once, then type on the new line: the word is **not** repeated; the same in a comment and a list item.
94. **Ready note name (0.9.69, D264).** A folder with "Audio 1", "Audio 2", "Audio 4": New note → Audio: the title is "Audio 3", Create at once makes it; again → "Audio 5". Choose Budget → "Budget 1"; type "Trip", choose another kind: "Trip" stays; empty the field, choose a kind: its ready name comes back. A folder of tasks: New → "Task 1". Russian: «Аудио 3», «Запись камеры 1».
93. **Change comment and one-recording limit (0.9.68, D262, D263).** A budget item → Change: "Comment (optional, goes into the log)", type "bakery", + 5 → Save: the log line ends with «bakery»; without a comment the line is as before. An audio note: under Record "Change for this recording" → 30 seconds → Save: the line says "30 s (this recording only)"; Record: it stops after 30 s; the next recording has 4 min again. The same for camera video and screen recording.
92. **Folders of tasks (0.9.67, D239).** New folder → "Folder of tasks" (the icon becomes a tick) → Create. In it "New": the dialog is "New task" with a title only. The task: View shows "In progress", start – end, comment, reminders, description; Edit: Done tick, comment, Start / End (date then time; an end before the start is refused), "Reminder…" opens the reminders of this task, description. An end in the past and not done: "Overdue" in red, also on the card. Calendar: a bar in the task's colour on every day from start to end, pale when done; the day list shows start – end. Appearance → Backgrounds has "Task". An old backup restores its folders as folders of notes; a new one keeps the kind.
91. **Recording in the background (0.9.66, D255).** An audio note: Record, lock the phone for a minute, unlock: still recording, the clip is a minute long; the notification "Notio is recording" has Stop, the red floating button (when allowed) stops it too. A camera video note: Record, press Home, wait, come back: still recording; lock the phone: the video goes on (check the file plays and has the whole time); the floating Stop works. Without recording the camera turns off when the app is minimised. A screen recording still stops on lock.
90. **Tool panel and sheet size (0.9.65, D261, D260).** A drawing → Edit: the panel lies over the bottom of the sheet; the frame icon with the band on the right moves it to the right (and back), kept after reopening; the arrow icon folds it into a round pencil button, which opens it again. Pinch: the sheet goes down to 25% (it stays in the middle) and up to 800%; tabs, zoom buttons and the panel stay on top. The percentage: the whole sheet in the free part. The frame icon with corners: "Sheet size": 16:9 → "= 1920 × 1080 px" → Save: the sheet is wide, the picture at the top left; cm: 21 × 29,7 → 1240 × 1754 px; 400 cm → "Too large", Save off. A blueprint the same; export PNG and the card show the new proportions.
89. **Search in a formatted Note (0.9.64, D256).** A Note with "Green tea / Milk / more tea": the magnifier → "tea": "1 / 2", both marked in View, the next arrow scrolls to the second; Edit: the same two marked in the field and the current one selected; the ticks "Aa" and "Regexp" work as in Markdown.
88. **The sheet of a drawing (0.9.63, D241).** A phone: a drawing → Edit: the sheet is larger than before, the tools lie over its bottom on their own background; two fingers move the sheet up to reach its bottom; "Hide the tools": the whole sheet shows, "Tools" brings them back (the sheet goes back into place). A tablet in landscape: the tools stand at the right, the sheet is as high as the area and nothing covers it. A blueprint the same, with the layers panel. The percentage button = the whole sheet (Fit).
87. **Search in the trash and in the calendar (0.9.62, D253, D252).** Trash with a deleted text note "Shopping" (text "bread and green tea"), a deleted folder with notes, a deleted photo with a comment: the magnifier → "tea": "1 / 1", the piece "bread and green tea" under the note, "tea" marked; the name of a note of the deleted folder and the photo's comment are found too; the arrows scroll to each. Calendar with a reminder three months ahead and a budget merge: the magnifier → part of the note name: "Found in the calendar" lists them with dates; a tap or the arrows show the month and the day.
86. **Budget merge (0.9.61, D237).** Two budgets: "Wallet" (Food 1000) and "Shop". Shop → Edit → "What this budget is": Expense; account Wallet; date and time two minutes ahead; items "food" 300 and "Toys" 200. The calendar shows 💰 on that day. Leave the app: at the time Shop goes to the trash, Wallet has Food 700 and Toys −200, both logs have a line. Wallet → Log → "Undo the merge": Food 1000 again, no Toys, Shop back from the trash with no time set. Then "Merge now" (with a confirmation), the switch "by the path group / item", and the phone restarted before the time (the merge runs at the time, or at the next start).
85. **A new note (0.9.60, D257).** A folder with many notes, scrolled down: New note → a title → Create: the list goes to the top and the new note has a coloured frame for a moment; with the sort "My order" it is first too; with "Name" the list goes to where it is.
84. **App log settings (0.9.59, D254).** Settings → Help and about → App log: "Keep an app log" on; ticks DEBUG, INFO, WARN, ERROR, CRITICAL all on, FATAL ticked and grey; "Clear by itself": once a day chosen. Untick INFO, make a screen recording: the log has no INFO lines of it, WARN / CRITICAL ones still come. Turn the log off: nothing new appears (a crash would still be written). Choose "never": the log keeps its lines past a day.
83. **Background restriction (0.9.58, D249).** Phone settings → Apps → Notio → Battery → Restricted: Settings → Recordings says "Work in the background: restricted" in red with "Open the settings"; a screen recording note: Record asks first (Open the settings / Record anyway); "Record anyway" and leave the app: the recording stops and a message explains why. Set Battery → Unrestricted: the line turns "allowed", no question, the recording goes on in the background.
82. **Screen recording with the app lock (0.9.57, D249; superseded by 83).** App lock on (as on the phone). A screen recording note: turn "Floating Stop button" back on; record 30 s with system + microphone: it must go on; Stop from the button or the notification; the video is saved. Then open Recents: Notio's picture is hidden again (the protection is back). The App log shows "the window is not secure" at the start and "secure" after. If it still stops, send the log.
81. **Recording limits (0.9.56, D251).** Settings → Recordings: three lines (audio, camera video, screen), 4 and "minutes" chosen. Set audio to 10 seconds: in an audio note the line says "Stops by itself after 10 s", Record says it again, after 10 s the recording stops and is in the list with "The time limit was reached …". Camera video 15 s: the same, the video is saved and plays. Screen recording 20 s (floating button off): minimise the app, wait: after 20 s the notification says it is saved. 0: "No time limit". Stop before the limit: no message later.
80. **Screen recording without the floating button (0.9.56, D249).** After the self-healing of 0.9.55 the switch under the sound choice is off; record the screen for 30 s with "no sound", then with "system + microphone"; send the App log. If it still stops at once, it is not the button.
79. **Screen recording on the Samsung (0.9.55, D249).** A screen recording note: under the sound choice "Floating Stop button" (on). Record for 30 s with the button: (a) the recording goes on — the fix works; or (b) it stops at once and a message says the button is turned off; then record again: no floating button, the recording goes on, Stop from the notification. Either way send Settings -> About -> App log. Turning the switch off by hand: the hint about the notification appears.
78. **Quick scroll (0.9.54, D250).** A folder with 40+ notes: scroll a little: two round buttons bottom right, above "New note"; "To the end": the last note, that button goes; "To the beginning": back up; the buttons fade 2 - 3 s after the list stops. The same in a long list, budget, schedule, audio, photo / video / screen note, the View of a long Markdown and a long "Note". Edit of a text over 3 000 characters or 40 lines: the buttons stay; each moves the cursor to the start / the end and the field follows (the keyboard opens). A short list: no buttons.
77. **Search in recordings, photos, videos (0.9.53, D248).** An audio note with three recordings, one renamed "Interview" and one with the comment "about the interview": the magnifier, type "interview": "1 / 2", the name and the comment marked, the arrows scroll to each; "record" finds the default names ("Recording 1" …). The same in a photo, a camera video and a screen recording note. The ticks of D246 work here too.
76. **Regexp mistakes (0.9.52, D247).** Regexp on; type `*tea*`: red "Error in the expression, character 1: “*” repeats what stands before it …", and `*tea*` below with the first star marked. Also `(tea`, `tea)`, `[abc`, `a{x}`, `tea\`, `[я-а]`: each its own sentence in the app language and the right character marked. Switch the app language: the sentences follow. Type the first letters of a word once, slowly and with the keyboard suggestions: results appear at once (the user saw "nothing until typed again" — report what is typed and where if it happens again).
75. **Search ticks and patterns (0.9.51, D246).** In a note search and on the main screen: under the field "Aa any case" (on) and ".* Regexp" (off). "TEA" finds tea; tick off: only "TEA". `*Tasty*`, `Tast*`, `t?a` work without Regexp. With Regexp: `^Buy`, `\d+ kg`, `tea|coffee`; `*Tasty*` shows a red "The expression cannot be read" and finds nothing, no crash. `(a+)+b` over a long line of "a": no freeze. Help -> "Search: case, patterns, regexp" shows `\d` with its backslash.
74. **Search inside a note (0.9.50, D238).** A Markdown note with a word in several places (one in a checklist line, one bold): the magnifier at the top opens a field; type the word in any case: every place is marked, the current one stronger, "1 / n"; the arrows go through them and the View scrolls to each; Edit: the same marks, the current one selected; "No matches" for a word that is not there; the cross or Back closes the search (Back a second time leaves the note). A list: the words in names and comments. A budget with a closed group holding the word: the group opens while searching, the arrow shows it open; close the search: it is closed again as it was saved.
73. **Calendar (0.9.49, D236).** Main screen: "Notio by vibic" is a line of its own, under it the buttons Search, Calendar, clock, Trash, ⋮, Settings. Make a reminder for today, one for another day this month, a weekly one (Monday and Thursday) and a monthly one. Calendar: today is circled and chosen, its reminder is listed under the month; bells on the other days (the weekly one on every Monday and Thursday from its next time on, the monthly one on its day); a day with more than three shows "+n". Tap a day with a bell: a bubble with time and note name; tap the name: the note opens; Back returns to the calendar. Arrows and a swipe change the month; Today comes back. A finished reminder is paler. Change the app language: the month and day names follow.
72. **Dragging lands exactly (0.9.48, D245).** A Markdown note with 300 checklist lines ("- [ ] 1" … "- [ ] 300"). View, scroll to 250; drag 251 onto 255 and let go: it stands after 255, nothing else moved; drag two neighbours past each other: they stay swapped at once (not "back in place"). Take a line near the top and pull it down: the text must not run up. Hold a line at the bottom edge: the text scrolls down and the line goes on; pull back: it stops.
71. **Photos and recordings under stress (0.9.48, D244).** Take several photos in a row with every overlay on (on a phone with a big camera): no crash; each photo is kept. Record audio with the storage almost full: "recording failed" instead of a crash. Settings -> Database file -> import a file, then delete it before confirming (or pick one from a removed memory card): "import failed", the app keeps working.
70. **No freeze when dragging (0.9.47, D242).** Repeat item 69 and drag slowly, stopping on the border between two lines: the app must not hang, the line stays on one side.
69. **Checklist lines in the View (0.9.46, D235).** A Markdown note with "- [ ] a", "- [x] b" with two indented sub-items "  - [ ] b1", "  - [ ] b2", and "- [ ] c". View: a, b, c have a four-arrow grip; drag b below c: b goes with b1 and b2; b1 can only change places with b2; a single item has no grip. The bin on b asks "Delete this line?" and says the lines under it go too; Delete removes b, b1, b2; Undo brings them back, Redo removes them again; moving then typing in Edit gives two separate undo steps. With a long list, dragging to the bottom edge scrolls the View.
68. **Blueprint order (0.9.45, D234).** A blueprint with three overlapping shapes: select the bottom one -> "Bring to front": it is drawn over the others; "Send to back": under them; Undo / Redo step through it. Layers: add three layers, ⋮ of the lowest -> "Move to the top": it is first in the list and its shapes are over the others; "Move to the bottom" puts it back; the items are greyed out on the top / bottom layer. Help -> Drawings and blueprints mentions both.
67. **New names (0.9.41, D227).** New note -> the kinds show "Заметка" (formatted) and "Заметка MD" (Markdown) in Russian, "Note" / "Markdown" in English; Settings -> Appearance -> backgrounds and the help say the same. Settings -> About -> Licences shows the SQLCipher and Roboto texts. Recovery mail with "no encryption" -> Save asks once more.

### 21.4 Verification build 0.7.0 (versionCode 10) - what to check

File: `dist/Notio-by-vibic777-0.7.0-debug.apk` (37.9 MB, 37918933 bytes, SHA-256 `7d1a9427ed47e149e0de7c17e265356a2a5908716c529c320b336dc4cd6285d0`), debug-signed with the same key, so it installs over 0.6.0 and keeps the data (the database is v5, unchanged since 0.6.0). Settings → About must say "Notio by vibic, version 0.7.0 (build 10)" and database version 5. Report what you tapped and what you expected; "works" for a line
is as valuable as "broken". **Take a full backup first** (Settings → Export all data). 0.7.0 differs from 0.6.0 in items 20-21 below (items 14-19 are the checks of 0.6.0, still open); items 12 and 13 are from 0.5.2, items 1-11 are the checks of 0.5.0 and 0.5.1.

1. **Font size.** Settings → Font size: the five levels change the text of the whole app at once; light, dark and the colour themes still look right; the level survives a restart.
2. **Zoom in notes.** Open a text note, a list and a budget: the corner has **− / 100% / +**; the buttons and a two-finger pinch make the letters of that note bigger or smaller,
   scrolling with one finger still works, a tap on the percentage returns to 100 %. Leaving the note forgets the zoom.
3. **Zoom in drawings.** In a drawing and a blueprint (View and Edit) pinch the sheet, move it with two fingers, use −/+/percent. In Edit draw on the zoomed sheet: the line must
   appear under the finger and a second finger must not leave a stray dot.
4. **Blueprint and layers.** New note → *Blueprint*. In Edit draw shapes; tap **Layers**: add a layer, draw on it, hide it (its shapes vanish), lock a layer (its shapes cannot be picked or drawn on),
   move a layer up/down and see the drawing order change, rename, delete a layer (asks first), move a selected shape to another layer (layer ⋮ menu). Export the blueprint as PNG: hidden
   layers must be missing. A drawing (not blueprint) has only the raster tools; a drawing made earlier with vector shapes still shows them.
5. **Reminders.** The clock icon on the main screen: add a reminder for a note two minutes ahead with sound and a pop-up, wait: **does the notification come, with sound, at the right time,
   also with the screen off?** Then try "ask for confirmation" + "repeat until confirmed", the Done button, edit, cancel, delete. On Android 13+ the notification permission is asked when
   the screen opens; if the app says exact alarms are not allowed, use its button to open the system setting. Restart the phone with a pending reminder: it must still fire.
6. **App lock.** Settings → App lock: turn it on with a password and a security question, put the app in the background for longer than the timeout and come back: the lock screen must cover
   everything; a wrong password five times makes you wait; "I forgot it" + the right answer sets a new password. Try the **pattern** and **login + password**, and fingerprint/face if the phone has it.
   With the lock on, a screenshot must be blocked and the recent-apps picture blank. Turn the lock off again with the current secret. *If you forget the secret and did not set a question, only
   reinstalling helps: keep the backup.*
7. **Backgrounds.** Settings → Backgrounds: give the whole app a picture and a folder-card strip a colour, a text note another picture; the text must stay readable; Reset returns the default.
8. **Sorting and folder order.** Main screen ⋮ → Sort: notes by name/colour/dates, ascending and descending; folders by name; with "My order" move a folder with its ⋮ menu.
9. **Featured note.** A folder card's ⋮ → Featured note…: choose a budget with Sum and Remainder in Full layout: the card must show them and update when the budget changes; choose a text note: only the title.
10. **From 0.4.1:** the search icon is highlighted while searching, Back and *Cancel search* end it, the Tags kind of search; the budget quantity field (tap and type, − and +); the black, gray and
    white themes; the audio bug (the next recording must be its own new card); the name *Notio by vibic* and "CyberNotio 2077 edition" in About; TXT export of a text note and a list.
11. **New in 0.5.1:** the launcher icon is **blue** with the white note, and the red text *vibic* (upper left) and *777* (lower right) over it: is it readable and does it look right on your launcher (round and square masks)? Settings -> About says "Developer: vibic777 + the AIs" in English; switch to Russian / French / German: "ИИшки", "les IA", "die KIs" - tell me which wording you like.
12. **Blueprint tools (the reported bug).** New note → Blueprint → Edit. Tap the tool chips one after another (Ellipse, Rectangle, Fill, Line...): **the highlighted chip must change at every tap**, and the shape drawn must be the one chosen. Choose Select and tap a drawn shape: a dashed frame appears; choose Fill and tap inside a closed shape: it is filled. Tell me exactly what still fails.
13. **Notification opens the note.** Add a reminder for a note two minutes ahead; when the notification comes, **tap it: the note itself must open** (with the lock on: first the lock screen, then the note). Try with the app closed and with it open on another screen.
14. **Lock timeout.** Settings → App lock → timeout: the presets are still there; **Other...** asks for minutes and seconds (try 0 min 30 s and 10 min 0 s); after leaving the app for that time the lock screen must come. In the password form, type two different passwords: the mismatch must be shown at once under the field and the button must stay off.
15. **Recovery by e-mail.** With the lock on: Recovery → **SMTP settings** (your host, port, encryption none / STARTTLS / SSL-TLS, login, password, from, to), send the test message, then lock the app, tap "I forgot it", choose e-mail: **does the code arrive, does it let you set a new secret, is a wrong code refused, does a second code before 60 s wait?** Try every encryption mode your server has.
16. **Recovery by an authenticator app.** Recovery → Authenticator: scan the QR code with Google Authenticator or similar (or type the key), enter the first code to confirm; then lock the app and recover with the current code. Also check that the security question still works.
17. **Reminder sound.** In the reminder dialog, the melody row: **Listen**, **Phone sounds** (pick a ringtone / an alarm sound), **My file** (pick an mp3 from the phone), Reset. Set a reminder two minutes ahead: **the notification must now play the chosen sound** (this was a defect). Check with the phone on silent / vibrate too, and after "Do not disturb".
18. **Alarm-style repeats, volume, fade-in.** A reminder with "Repeat like an alarm clock": 2 more rings every 1 minute: three rings in total, then it stops; with confirmation on, the rings continue until Done. Volume 50 % and fade-in 30 s: the melody must start quietly, rise and stop rising at the chosen level; the notification's **Silence** action stops it; tapping the notification opens the note and stops the sound. Note if the sound plays from the service or from the channel (both are fine) and whether a reboot with a pending reminder still rings.
19. **Recurrence.** Every day: after it rings, the reminder card shows tomorrow; chosen weekdays (e.g. Mon and Thu); every month on day N (try 31: a month without the 31st is skipped, the card must show the next month that has it). Old reminders keep working as they did. Then **About**: "Notio by vibic", version 0.6.0 (build 9), and the file name of the APK.
20. **New-note dialog on the tablet (D135).** Home -> New note: **every kind must show its name** (Text, List, Budget, Drawing, Blueprint, Audio); with the tablet in landscape the dialog must scroll and the last kinds must be reachable.
21. **Ten languages (D132).** Settings -> Language: English, Russian, Belarusian, French, German, Spanish, Italian, Chinese, Japanese, Korean. Switch to each: the whole app (main screen, the note editors, Settings, Help, the lock and the reminder dialog) must be in that language, with no cut-off text or missing glyphs; note anything that reads oddly (a native reader is still needed). Then go back to your own language.

| 2026-09-22 | **Build 0.9.21 (D187) — urgent fix.** The person installed 0.9.20, the encryption attempt from D186 failed as before, they closed the app, and it then **crashed on every launch**. Root cause found: `DatabaseEncryptionStore.readBlocking()` returned the stored passphrase unconditionally, not gated on the `enabled` flag the way its own `enabled` return value already was — so a passphrase left over from a failed `generatePassphrase()` (always saved before the conversion runs) leaked into `AppContainer.database`'s `AppDatabase.create(context, passphrase)` call, which then tried to open the still-plaintext file through SQLCipher and failed every time. Fixed at the source: `passphrase` is now `null` whenever `enabled` is `false`, matching. New test `DatabaseEncryptionStoreTest` (3 cases) pins the scenario. **694 tests, 0 failures, BUILD SUCCESSFUL** (`./gradlew :app:compileDebugKotlin :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug`, JDK 21). APK: `dist/Notio-by-vibic777-0.9.21-debug.apk` (59247749 bytes, SHA-256 `50833f68cb8c3382dc1cc2d9885ccb1b07d80b5f567121bc3603b08ea24df4c3`); installing it over the crashing 0.9.20 (no uninstall, no data wipe) should recover the app, since the underlying database file itself was never touched by the failed attempt. **Not yet confirmed by the person on the actual affected phone** — this is the next thing to hear back on. |
| 2026-09-22 | **Build 0.9.22 (D188).** 0.9.21 confirmed by the person: the app stopped crash-looping, but encryption itself still failed — and this time the D186 diagnostic text showed the real cause: `UnsatisfiedLinkError: no implementation found for ... net.zetetic.database.sqlcipher.SQLiteConnection.native...`. Diagnosed without a device by unzipping the built APK and the `sqlcipher-android` AAR directly: `libsqlcipher.so` was genuinely present for all four ABIs, correctly packaged — not a missing-library problem. The real cause: this library does not load its native code automatically; its own README shows `System.loadLibrary("sqlcipher")` at the start of every usage example, which this project never called anywhere. Fixed: `core/database/SqlCipherNativeLibrary.kt` (`ensureLoaded()`), called before all three places that touch SQLCipher (`DatabaseEncryptor`, `RawDatabaseTransfer`, `AppDatabase.create`). No new automated test — this is a real-device-only failure mode, not something JVM/Robolectric can exercise. **694 tests, 0 failures, BUILD SUCCESSFUL** (`./gradlew :app:compileDebugKotlin :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug`, JDK 21). APK: `dist/Notio-by-vibic777-0.9.22-debug.apk` (59247749 bytes, SHA-256 `3e6dfe9cb28d816c0bade45c09ed58756f11a3e42739dd56582ef624df094962`). **Not yet confirmed on the person's phone that encryption itself now actually works** — this is the next thing to hear back on. |

| 2026-09-22 | **Build 0.9.26 (D193).** The person reported a real crash: on cold start, the folder strip briefly showed only the "New folder" button, folders then appeared, they started scrolling the strip, and the app crashed. No stack trace was copied this time, only the description, so this is diagnosed by inspection, not from evidence as strong as D189/D190's copied error text — flagged as such. Cause read from the existing (pre-this-session) `HomeScreen.kt` folder-strip auto-scroll `LaunchedEffect` (D163): it computes `index` from `state.folders`, then suspends on `snapshotFlow { rowState.layoutInfo.viewportSize.width }.first { it > 0 }`, then calls `rowState.scrollToItem(index, -middle)` — if the folder list changed during that suspend (plausible here, with the encrypted database still catching up on cold start) `index` can be stale, and `LazyListState.scrollToItem` does not clamp an out-of-range index, it throws `IndexOutOfBoundsException`; a concurrent manual scroll on the same `LazyListState` is a second, independent way the same call can misbehave. Fixed, defense in depth: re-check `index` against a freshly-read `rowState.layoutInfo.totalItemsCount` right after the suspend point before using it, and wrap the `scrollToItem` call itself in `runCatching` since a scroll-position convenience should never be allowed to crash the app. No dedicated new test — Robolectric's synchronous test clock does not reproduce this kind of Compose scroll-state race; verified by code review, not by reproducing the crash. **694 tests, 0 failures, BUILD SUCCESSFUL** (`./gradlew :app:compileDebugKotlin :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug`, JDK 21). APK: `dist/Notio-by-vibic777-0.9.26-debug.apk` (59285213 bytes, SHA-256 `ca6f0f62ba66f43d2a5e6bcabc2a009506c1527efb6edf35bdc0cb71aa606435`). **Not yet confirmed on the person's phone that the crash is actually gone** — since this was diagnosed from a description rather than a stack trace, treat it as the most likely cause found by inspection, watch for recurrence. |

## 23. What the app does today (functional inventory; 0.5.0 text, extended by §23.11 - §23.12 up to version 0.9.7)

This section describes the behaviour that exists in the code and is covered by JVM tests unless it says otherwise. "Not run on a device"
applies to everything except what §0.2 lists as confirmed by the user. Items still to build are listed in §21.3, not here.

### 23.1 Platform, install, versions

- Android 9 (API 28) up to Android 17 (compiled against API 37, `targetSdk 36`); phones only; light and dark theme (follows the system or is
  chosen in Settings); package `com.notebudget.app`; the debug APK is signed with the developer's debug key, so a new APK installs over an old one.
- Version 0.8.0, build 11 (D97, D125, D131), application name "Notio by vibic". Settings → About shows: version and build, "Developer: vibic777 + the AIs" (RU "ИИшки", FR "les IA", DE "die KIs"), "CyberNotio 2077 edition", "Made with love for my daughter Katerina",
  and the versions of the backup format (1) and the database (6).
- **Ten languages** (EN default, RU, BE, FR, DE, ES, IT, ZH, JA, KO) for the app, the help and the glossary (D128, D132); a test fails the build if a string, plural or help text is missing or left in English.

### 23.2 Folders and the main screen

- First launch: onboarding creates the first folder (name, colour, one of 6 styles, optional emoji, live preview; default name "My Notes").
- Combined main screen: folder cards (6 styles, note count) and the notes of the selected folder. Folders are flat, names are unique (case-insensitive),
  the last folder cannot be deleted. Editing a folder: name, colour, style, icon.
- Deleting a folder asks what to do with its notes (move them to another folder, or trash them with the folder) and can be undone at once.
- Note cards: title (bold if chosen), pin, favourite, type, a **rendered preview** (formatted text, ☐ / ☑ boxes, bullets; never raw Markdown); a menu with pin,
  favourite, bold title, move to another folder, delete. Order: pinned, favourite, most recently changed.
- Top bar: **search**, **reminders (clock)**, **trash (with a count badge)**, ⋮ (**sort**, import a note from a file), settings. Folder cards show the folder's **featured note** and, for a budget, its chosen values (D123); the notes and the folders are ordered by the chosen sort (D123).
- **Search (D109):** a panel with a Notes / Folders switch. Notes: words (all must match) in the title, text and tag names of the notes of ALL folders, plus tag chips (a note needs all chosen
  tags); results keep the colour of their own folder. Folders: narrows the folder cards by name. **Tags:** ⋮ on a card → Tags (tick or type new, any number), shown on the card; Settings → Manage tags.

### 23.3 Note kinds (a registry; a new kind is one class, D82)

- **Text (TEXT):** Markdown. Always opens in *View* (D108); *Edit* is the second tab. Edit: toolbar (bold, italic, strikethrough, heading levels,
  bullets, numbering, check boxes, quote, inline code, code block, link, rule), Enter continues a list, and bold/italic/strikethrough/code are styled
  while typing (the marks are dimmed). Preview: rendered blocks with tappable check boxes. Autosave.
- **List (LIST, D92/D96):** items with a check box, text, optional comment, quantity (exact, up to 3 decimals) and unit; **unit totals** ("Total by unit"): a row with
  a name, comment and unit whose result is the sum of the quantities of all items in that unit (checked or not); several totals with different units.
  Old plain check-box lists still open. **Opens in View, where the check boxes can be ticked; Edit changes everything else (D99).** **Price mode (D101):** each item also has a price of one
  and a price unit (any unit); cost = quantity × price; totals per unit and per price unit (e.g. 5 pcs, 400 rub); unit-total rows also show the cost of their items. Export: JSON, Markdown, TXT, PDF.
- **Budget (BUDGET):** a tree of groups and items; an item has a sum and goal **for one piece** and a **quantity** (1-9999); totals cascade to every group and the root
  (remainder = goal − sum, may be negative); comments; collapse; add, edit, delete with confirmation; per-note display options (total at the bottom,
  group totals below the items, newest first); **the group of an item or group is chosen when adding and changed in Edit (D102)**. Opens in View (no row menus, but **the "Change" +/- button on every item is there in View and in Edit, D164**).
- **Drawing (DRAWING, D98):** since 0.5.0 a raster sheet only (a vector layer of an older drawing is still shown, read-only); the description below of the vector tools now belongs to the **Blueprint (BLUEPRINT)**, which has vector shapes on **layers** (add, rename, order, hide, lock, delete, move a shape to a layer) and no raster sheet. Both open in View first and **zoom** (100 %-800 %, buttons and two fingers, pan with two fingers). The old text: one screen with two layers. *Raster:* pencil, brush, eraser (a ring of its real size is drawn), bucket fill, colours, width, undo, autosave.
  *Vector:* line, arrow, rectangle, ellipse, triangle, pen; select (tap inside a shape), move, drag end handles, fill, outline and fill colours, duplicate,
  delete, forward/backward, undo; each layer can be hidden. **Colours: ready-made swatches, recent colours and an RGB picker (sliders or `#RRGGBB`).**
  Export: PNG, JPEG (both layers merged) or "Layers" (JSON with editable shapes).
- **Audio (AUDIO, D112):** a list of recordings; *Record* adds one (microphone permission asked on first use); each has a name, a comment, its own play/slider, export as `.m4a` and delete; the
  note exports one `.m4a` or a `.zip` of all (named "note title - recording name"). Notes from before keep their one recording as the first entry.
- **Unknown kinds** from a newer app are kept and shown with a placeholder.

### 23.4 Trash (D93)

- Deleting a note or folder moves it to the trash (undo at once). The trash screen lists folders (with their note count) and notes with the deletion date
  and days left; **Restore** (a note whose folder is trashed asks for a folder), **Delete forever** (confirmed; removes reminders, budget rows, alarms and
  drawing/audio files), **Empty trash** (confirmed). Retention: 7/14/30/60/90 days or never, expired items are removed at every app start.
- Ways in: the bin icon on the main screen (with a count), and Settings → Open trash.

### 23.5 Units of measurement (D92, D96)

- Built-in, never deletable: pieces, kilograms, litres, metres, roubles, US dollars, pounds sterling, euros (localized symbol and name).
  Your own units: add (1-24 characters, unique), delete only when no list (trashed lists included) uses them, with confirmation.
  A list carries the names of the custom units it uses, so it reads right on another phone.

### 23.6 Settings

- Language (system, EN, RU, FR, DE), light / dark / system, **ten colour themes (green, pink, orange, red, light blue, blue, lime, black, gray, white; D105, D115)**, **font size (small ... extra large, D119)**, **backgrounds (D110)**, **app lock (D124)**, reminder notification privacy (default: generic text),
  trash retention, units, **tags**, full backup export/import, help and glossary, About. A note's own colour: ⋮ → Colour (D106).

### 23.7 Export, import, backup (D84, D85)

- **Full backup:** one ZIP with all folders, notes (trashed too), budget rows, reminders, drawings, audio and non-secret settings (language, theme, privacy,
  trash retention, custom units); no passwords or hashes. Import modes: merge (newer wins), add as new copies, replace (asks first); everything is
  checked (missing folders, broken links, unsafe file names), all-or-nothing, totals recalculated.
- **Single note:** TEXT `.md`, `.txt`, `.pdf`; LIST JSON with structure, a text snapshot (md), `.txt` or `.pdf`; BUDGET JSON with structure or a text snapshot; DRAWING PNG/JPEG/Layers;
  AUDIO `.m4a` or `.zip` of all recordings. Tags travel by name in backups and JSON files. Import recognises the type by the file; an imported note always gets a new id and the chosen folder.
- **Compatibility is mandatory:** every field has a default, unknown fields are ignored, unknown note kinds are kept, formats carry a version, and golden
  files of old formats are tests that must keep passing.

### 23.8 Reminders and security (D122, D124)

- **Reminders:** tapping a reminder notification **opens the note** (D126); the reminder screen (clock icon; or a note's ⋮ → Reminders): add, edit, cancel, delete, confirm; note, day and time, sound / vibration / pop-up, confirmation, repeat until confirmed; exact alarm with an inexact
  fallback and a note that opens the system setting; notification permission asked on Android 13+. Engine: restore after reboot and time changes, notification channels (one per sound and vibration), privacy levels (Settings). **Since 0.6.0 (D129, D130):** the melody is chosen like an alarm clock (system sounds or the person's own file, Listen, Reset); the notification plays it; extra rings every N minutes X times; volume 10-100 % with a fade-in of 10-120 s (own foreground service when needed, Silence action); recurrence every day / chosen weekdays / day of the month (a month without the day is skipped). No snooze yet.
- **App lock:** password, 3×3 pattern, or login + password; optional fingerprint/face as an addition; timeout in presets or any minutes + seconds (D129); recovery by the security question, by a code e-mailed through the user's own SMTP server and by an authenticator app (TOTP, QR code); the only network use of the app is that one mail (permission `INTERNET`); a wait after five wrong tries; hashes only, in private storage, never in a backup;
  screenshots blocked while it is on (D124). No roles, no e-mail recovery.
- **Backgrounds (D110), font size (D119), zoom (D118):** see Settings and the notes above.

### 23.9 Data and quality

- Room database, schema v4 (unchanged in 0.5.0; v1 → v2 adds `note.extras`, v2 → v3 adds `budget_node.quantity`, v3 → v4 adds `tag` and `note_tag`), migrations tested from the exported schema files;
  drawings, audio clips and the vector layer live in files, not in the database. Money is `Long` minor units, quantities are `Long` thousandths, times are UTC.
- 424 JVM tests: business rules, view models, real in-memory Room (cascades, foreign keys), backup and file round trips, golden-file compatibility,
  migrations, translations, and Robolectric UI flows on the real `MainActivity`. **No test has run on a real Android version.**

### 23.10 Changing the design (question from the developer's daughter, 2026-09-20)

Design changes are safe: the UI is Jetpack Compose with Material 3, and the data, business rules and storage know nothing about how things look
(MVVM, §9). What is easy, what is a little work, and what to watch:

- **Easy (one to three files, no risk to data):** colours, fonts, corner shapes and the overall mood - the theme is in `core/ui/theme/`
  (`Color.kt`, `Type.kt`, `Theme.kt`); today only the primary colour is set (a green), the rest is the Material 3 default, so a new palette, a
  different font or rounder cards is a small change. Light and dark themes already exist and would follow.
- **A little work (per screen):** layouts, spacing, icons, card styles. The spacing numbers (`dp`) are written in the screens (about 140 places), there is no
  shared "design tokens" file yet; the first design task should be to gather colours, spacing and shapes into one place, after which a redesign is quick.
  The six folder styles (`feature/folders`) are their own small design system and can be changed or extended independently.
- **What to watch:** (1) UI tests find things by their text and description ("Restore", "Trash, 1 item"), so renaming a label means updating a test, not
  the app logic; (2) every text lives in the string files in ten languages, a new label means ten translations (a test checks they are all there);
  (3) we cannot see the screen ourselves: no emulator on this PC, so a redesign is judged on the phone by the QA (the user and the daughter), best with
  screenshots or a sketch of what is wanted; (4) a big redesign should be one small step at a time and each step built as a package, like the checklist in §21.4.
- **Possible next step (not started):** a "Design" setting with a few colour themes to choose from, and the tokens file above.

### 23.11 New in 0.8.0 (D134, D136 - D149)

- **Three more note kinds:** Photo, Camera video, Screen recording (multi-notes with thumbnails, full-screen viewer, in-note video, selection mode, export, backup up to 40 MB per file); overlays of time, place, text and picture; the place of a picture and (optionally) of an audio recording.
- **Order:** twelve sort keys, own order by dragging a grip, pins by time for notes and folders, timestamps of creation and change everywhere in one global date / time format, naming of exported files.
- **Settings:** pages with a search; trash never empties itself by default, and the trash links to its setting; backup password; "Show" in every password field.
- **Small fixes:** zoom beside View / Edit, the text note View first, folder colours with RGB.
- Everything above: JVM-tested for the data side, **not run on a device**.

### 23.12 New in 0.8.1 - 0.9.7 (D150 - D164)

- **Reminders:** snooze (1 - 60 min, survives a reboot), the alarm-clock screen over the lock screen, two buttons only (Repeat / Turn off or Done), the notification removed after Turn off, a quiet "Reminders are on" notification while any reminder is set, an alarm test button. **Not confirmed on Android 14.**
- **Schedule (SCHEDULE, the tenth kind, D155):** time rows and calculators of the difference between two times (next-day rule, "?" for a missing time), drag grips in View and Edit (also on the rows of list notes), zoom, export JSON / TXT / PDF.
- **Media trash (D156, database v8):** deleted photos, videos, screen recordings and audio clips stay as files and can be restored, exported or deleted for good; only the list of the media trash travels in a backup.
- **Cards and order:** four time-stamp switches (D157), a sort of the notes per folder with "Apply to all folders" (D158), preview of 0 - 2 lines with recording names (D159), card pictures none / first / deck of three and a switch for drawings, drawing preview on white paper (D160, D161), the app opens on the last folder, New folder / New note in the ⋮ menu (D161), the folder card with three buttons on top (D162), the strip scrolls to the open folder (D163), the note kinds sorted by name in the new-note dialog.
- **Folder card** no longer shows a featured note; the "Featured note" menu item is gone (D151), old data is kept.
- **Blueprint:** a fill is saved at once (D162).
- **Budget:** the "Change" +/- button on items, in View and in Edit (D164), with the "Enter" choice (D165).
- **List (D165, 0.9.8):** items and unit totals are cards; adding and editing go through dialogs; two Change buttons (Quantity, Price) on each item in View and in Edit.
- **Numbers (0.9.8):** database v8, backup format 1, 604 JVM / Robolectric tests, 10 languages. Everything above: JVM-tested for the data side, **not run on a device**.
- **Known gaps:** the in-app help does not mention the schedule, the media trash, card pictures or the Change button, and the "Notes" help still says five kinds; TalkBack pass; native review of the translations.
- **Budget log (D166, 0.9.9, database v9):** a log per budget note (added, changed sum / goal / quantity, renamed, moved, deleted), shown newest first, saved as TXT or PDF, clearable, kept in backups.
- **Select in the folder menu (D167):** check boxes on the notes of one folder, Select all, Clear, Delete (N) with Undo, Done.
- **Time stamps are off by default (D168).**
- **Reminder bell on the note card (D169, 0.9.10):** only the bell, no details.
- **Currency of a budget (D170, 0.9.11):** a list of currencies (14 built-in + the person's own), one per budget note, written after every sum.
- **Streamed backups (D174, 0.9.13):** any size (files up to 2 GB, archive up to 3.5 GB), a waiting dialog with a warning and a file counter; a bad file is skipped and counted.
- **Budget dragging (D176, 0.9.14):** groups and items move among their brothers by a grip, in View and in Edit.
- **Comments (D177, 0.9.15, database v10):** on notes, folders and budget rows; cards show the first 16 characters as the last line; switches in Settings and in the budget display options.
- **Sorting in budgets and lists (D178, 0.9.16):** by name, date, sum, goal, remainder, progress (budget) and name, date, quantity, price, cost, unit, done (list); the own order is kept.
- **Cloning (D179, 0.9.17):** ⋮ -> Clone on any note; `title_clone1`, `_clone2` ...; its own copy of files and budget rows.
- **0.9.18 (D180 - D183):** the favourite star is gone (a pin is the only mark, database v11); backgrounds for all ten kinds of notes; sorting of schedules; collapse / expand all groups of a budget.
